SCHEDULES
Disclosure Schedule

EQUITY PURCHASE AGREEMENT
THIS EQUITY PURCHASE AGREEMENT
is made as of August 9, 2026, by and among (a) The Boeing Company, a Delaware corporation (“Seller”), (b) Wisk
Aero LLC, a Delaware limited liability company (“Wisk”), (c) Insitu, Inc., a Washington corporation (“Insitu
US”), (d) Insitu Pacific Pty Ltd, a proprietary company incorporated under the laws of the Commonwealth of Australia (“Insitu
Pacific”), (e) Wisk Australia Pty Ltd, a proprietary company incorporated under the laws of the Commonwealth of Australia
(“Wisk Australia”), (f) Boeing Emirates Ltd, a private company limited by shares incorporated under the laws
of Abu Dhabi Global Market (“Insitu Emirates”, and collectively with Wisk, Insitu US, Insitu Pacific and
Wisk Australia, the “Companies” and, each individually, a “Company”), and (g) Archer Aviation
Inc., a Delaware corporation (“Buyer”). Seller and Buyer are referred to collectively herein as the “Parties”
and each individually as a “Party.”
RECITALS
WHEREAS, Seller, or an Affiliate
of Seller, owns, beneficially and of record, (a) all of the issued and outstanding limited liability company interests of Wisk (the
“Wisk Interests”), (b) all of the issued and outstanding shares of common stock, par value $1.00 per share, of
Insitu US (the “Insitu US Shares”), (c) all of the issued and outstanding ordinary shares of capital stock of
Insitu Pacific (the “Insitu Pacific Shares”), (d) all of the issued and outstanding shares of capital stock of
Wisk Australia (the “Wisk Australia Shares”) and (e) all of the issued and outstanding shares of capital stock
of Insitu Emirates (the “Insitu Emirates Shares” and, collectively with the Wisk Interests, the Insitu US Shares,
the Insitu Pacific Shares and the Wisk Australia Shares, the “Purchased Interests”);
WHEREAS, Seller wishes to
sell, or cause to be sold, and Buyer wishes to purchase, or cause to be purchased, the Purchased Interests, on the terms and subject
to the conditions set forth in this Agreement;
WHEREAS, as part of the consideration
for the Purchased Interests Buyer will (a) issue and deliver to Seller shares of Class A Common Stock of Buyer, par value $0.0001
per share (the “Acquirer Shares”) in a private placement exempt from registration pursuant to Section 4(a)(2) of
the Securities Act and (b) deliver the Warrants to Seller; and
WHEREAS, the payment, issuance
and/or delivery of the Acquirer Shares and Warrants to Seller pursuant to this Agreement shall be treated as being made to the applicable
Affiliate of Seller and then transferred, directly or indirectly, to Seller.
NOW, THEREFORE, in consideration
of the foregoing premises and the mutual covenants, agreements and warranties herein contained, the receipt and sufficiency of which
are hereby acknowledged, the Parties agree as follows:
ARTICLE 1
Definitions; Interpretation
1.1 Definitions.
The following terms shall have the following meanings for the purposes of this Agreement:
“2024 Audited Financial
Statements” means, collectively, the 2024 Insitu Audited Financial Statements and the 2024 Wisk Audited Financial Statements.
“2024 Insitu Audited
Financial Statements” means audited carve-out combined statements of operations, statements of comprehensive income, balance
sheets, statements of cash flows and statements of equity and associated footnotes for Insitu US, Insitu Pacific and Insitu Emirates
and each of their respective Subsidiaries, as of and for the year ended December 31, 2024 prepared in accordance with GAAP.
“2024 Wisk Audited
Financial Statements” means audited carve-out combined statements of operations, statements of comprehensive income, balance
sheets, statements of cash flows and statements of equity and associated footnotes for Wisk, Wisk Australia and each of their respective
Subsidiaries, as of and for the year ended December 31, 2024 prepared in accordance with GAAP.
“2025 Audited Financial
Statements”. Means, collectively, the 2025 Insitu Audited Financial Statements and the 2025 Wisk Audited Financial Statements.
“2025 Insitu Audited
Financial Statements” means audited carve-out combined statements of operations, statements of comprehensive income, balance
sheets, statements of cash flows and statements of equity and associated footnotes, for Insitu US, Insitu Pacific and Insitu Emirates
and each of their respective Subsidiaries, as of and for the year ended December 31, 2025 prepared in accordance with GAAP.
“2025 Wisk Audited
Financial Statements” means audited carve-out combined statements of operations, statements of comprehensive income, balance
sheets, statements of cash flows and statements of equity and associated footnotes for Wisk, Wisk Australia and each of their respective
Subsidiaries, as of and for the year ended December 31, 2025 prepared in accordance with GAAP.
“2025 and 2024 Audited
Financial Statements” means, collectively, the 2025 Audited Financial Statements and 2024 Audited Financial Statements.
“2026 Audited Financial
Statements” means, collectively, the 2026 Insitu Audited Financial Statements and the 2026 Wisk Audited Financial Statements.
“2026 Insitu Audited
Financial Statements” means audited carve-out combined statements of operations, statements of comprehensive income, balance
sheets, statements of cash flows and statements of equity and associated footnotes for Insitu US, Insitu Pacific and Insitu Emirates
and each of their respective Subsidiaries, as of and for the year ended December 31, 2026, prepared in accordance with GAAP.
 2 
“2026 Wisk Audited
Financial Statements” means audited carve-out combined statements of operations, statements of comprehensive income, balance
sheets, statements of cash flows and statements of equity and associated footnotes for Wisk, Wisk Australia and each of their respective
Subsidiaries, as of and for the year ended December 31, 2026 prepared in accordance with GAAP.
“Accepting Employee”
has the meaning set forth in Section 7.2(c).
“Accounting Firm”
means a nationally recognized independent firm that (a) is capable of serving as an accounting expert with relevant experience,
(b) is not the regular accounting firm of Buyer or Seller or any of their respective Affiliates and (c) is mutually agreeable
to Buyer and Seller; provided that if Buyer and Seller are unable to so agree to such a firm, then Buyer and Seller shall each
select such a firm and such firms shall jointly select a third nationally recognized independent accounting firm.
“Accounting Principles”
means the accounting principles and procedures set forth in Exhibit A (Accounting Principles).
“Acquirer Shares”
has the meaning set forth in the Recitals.
“Action”
means any claim, action, suit, litigation, opposition, arbitration, investigation, audit or proceeding (whether civil, criminal or administrative)
commenced or brought by or before any court or other Governmental Authority or arbitrator.
“Active Government
Contracts” has the meaning set forth in Section 4.15(a)(i).
“Additional Listing
Application” has the meaning set forth in Section 7.15(a).
“ADGM Companies
Regulations” means the Companies Regulations 2020 of the Abu Dhabi Global Market, as amended, supplemented or restated from
time to time.
“AECA”
means the Arms Export Control Act.
“Aircraft Authorizations”
has the meaning set forth in Section 4.12(k).
“Alternative Transaction”
means any agreement, offer or proposal for, or any indication of interest in, (a) any acquisition of the Companies and their Subsidiaries
or all or substantially all of the assets of the Companies and their Subsidiaries or (b) any direct or indirect acquisition or purchase
of all or a majority of the equity interests of the Companies and their Subsidiaries, in each case, whether by way of a merger, consolidation,
asset sale, stock purchase, license, tender offer, reorganization or other business combination.
“Affiliate”
means, with respect to any specified Person, any other Person that, directly or indirectly, controls, is under common control with or
is controlled by, such specified Person, whether now or in the future. The term “control” (including the terms “controlling,”
“under common control with” and “controlled by”) means (a) the direct or indirect ownership of more than
50% of the stock or other equity interests having the right to vote for directors or managers thereof or (b) the possession, direct
or indirect, of the power to direct or cause the direction of the management or the policies of a Person, whether through the ownership
of outstanding voting securities, by contract or otherwise. Each Company shall be an Affiliate of (x) Seller prior to the Closing
and (y) Buyer from and after the Closing.

has the meaning set forth in Section 6.14(d).
“Agreed Portion”
has the meaning set forth in Section 10.4(b)(ii).
“Agreed Press Release”
has the meaning set forth in Section 7.3.
“Aggregate Consideration”
has the meaning set forth in Section 2.3.
“Agreement”
means this Agreement, including the Disclosure Schedule and all other exhibits and schedules hereto, as it and they may be amended, restated
or otherwise modified from time to time.
“AI Requirements”
means, in each case relating to the development, deployment, importation, and/or distribution of AI Technology: (a) each of
the Party’s own written policies; (b) all applicable Laws related to AI Technology; and (c) all Contracts into which
the Party has entered.
“AI Technology”
means any artificial intelligence systems or models and profiling or automated decision-making technology used, developed, deployed,
imported, and/or distributed by the Party.
“Anti-Corruption
Law” has the meaning set forth in Section 4.12(c).
“Antitrust Laws”
means all (a) antitrust or competition Laws, including the Sherman Act, the Clayton Act, the HSR Act, the Federal Trade Commission
Act, each as amended and including any regulations promulgated thereunder, and all other (whether U.S. or non-U.S.) supranational, international,
national, federal, state and local Laws, that are designed or intended to require notification of, or prohibit, restrict or regulate
actions having the purpose or effect of monopolization or restraint of trade or lessening of competition through merger or acquisition;
and (b) laws regulating national security or foreign direct investment.
“ASIC”
means the Australian Securities and Investments Commission.
“Associated Person”
means, with respect to a Party, any of such Party’s former, current and future Affiliates, directors, officers, employees, incorporators,
agents, financing sources, attorneys, consultants, equityholders, members, stockholders, shareholders, controlling Persons, Representatives,
managers, operating, general or limited partners or assignees (or any former, current or future Affiliates, directors, officers, employees,
incorporators, agents, financing sources, attorneys, consultants, equityholders, members, stockholders, shareholders, controlling Persons,
Representatives, managers, operating, general or limited partners or assignees of any of the foregoing).
“Australian Tax
Act” shall mean the Income Tax Assessment Act 1936 (Cth), the Income Tax Assessment Act 1997 (Cth) or the Taxation
Administration Act 1953 (Cth), as the context requires.

Value” means, as of any date of determination, an amount equal to: (a) the product of (i) the Buyer Fully Diluted
Share Number and (ii) the Buyer EV Per Share Price; plus (b) the absolute value of the Buyer Indebtedness; minus (c) the
Buyer Cash; determined in accordance with the procedures and definitions set forth on Exhibit B.

Party” has the meaning set forth in Section 10.1(a).
“Buyer Intellectual
Property” has the meaning set forth in Section 5.18(a).

“Buyer Material
Adverse Effect” means (a) Buyer having a Buyer Enterprise Value equal to or less than [***] for any five
(5) consecutive trading days or (b) a material adverse effect on the financial condition or results of operations of Buyer
and its Subsidiaries, taken as a whole and on a consolidated basis, when viewed on both a long-term and short-term basis; provided,
however, that none of the following (and no effect arising out of or resulting from any of the following) shall, either alone
or in combination with any other effect, constitute or be taken into account in determining whether a Buyer Material Adverse Effect has
occurred under this clause (b): (i) any general economic, business, political, industry, trade or credit, financial or capital
market condition (whether in the United States or internationally), including any condition affecting generally the industries or markets
in which Buyer and its Subsidiaries operate; (ii) any storm, earthquake, tsunami, tornado, hurricane, flood, mudslide, wild fire,
act of God or other force majeure event; (iii) any Public Health Event or any action or omission in respect of any Public Health
Measure; (iv) any act of war (whether declared or not declared), sabotage, cyberterrorism or other cyberattack (including a cyberattack
perpetrated by or sponsored by a Governmental Authority whether or not specifically targeted at Buyer or its Subsidiaries or the industry
in which Buyer or its Subsidiaries operate), terrorism, military action or the escalation of any of the foregoing; (v) any Order
or any actual or prospective change in Laws, regulations or accounting rules, including the interpretations thereof, or any change after
the date hereof in the interpretation or enforcement of any of the foregoing, or any new Law and any tariffs or other Taxes imposed under
Order or any actual or prospective change in any Law, or any change in general legal, regulatory, trade or political conditions; (vi) any
formal statement by or on behalf of the U.S. White House; (vii) any strike, slowdown or work stoppage, or any sequester, shutdown,
default or similar event by or involving a Governmental Authority; (viii) any bankruptcy, insolvency or other financial distress
of any customer, supplier or other counterparty of Buyer or its Subsidiaries; (ix) the taking of any action expressly required or
expressly permitted to be taken by Buyer by this Agreement or the Related Agreements; (x) the negotiation, entry into or public
announcement of this Agreement or pendency of the transactions contemplated by this Agreement, including (1) any claim, suit, action
or proceeding in connection with the transactions contemplated by this Agreement or (2) any action taken in connection with obtaining
regulatory consents including the actions required to be taken by Seller pursuant to Section 6.6; (xi) the breach of
this Agreement or the Confidentiality Agreement by Seller or any Company (or an Affiliate of Seller or any Company, if applicable) to
the extent such breach has proximately resulted in the failure of any condition set forth in Section 8.2 to be satisfied;
(xii) any losses of employees, customers, suppliers or other counterparties of Buyer or any Subsidiary of Buyer as a result of the
execution and delivery or performance of this Agreement or the Related Agreements or the transactions contemplated hereby or thereby
or the identity of Seller or the Companies or any communication regarding plans or intentions with respect to the business conducted
by Buyer and its Subsidiaries; (xiii) any delay or cancellation of orders for products or services in connection with taking any
action or omitting to take any action at the express request of, or with the express approval of, Seller; (xiv) any action required
to be taken or required to be omitted to be taken under Law; (xv) any failure by Buyer or its Subsidiaries to meet any projections,
estimates or budgets for any period prior to, on or after the date of this Agreement; and (xvi) any change in the market price or
trading volume of the Acquirer Shares (it being understood that, solely with respect to the foregoing clauses (xv) and (xvi),
the facts, events or circumstances giving rise to such change may themselves constitute or be taken into account in determining whether
a Buyer Material Adverse Effect has occurred under this clause (b) if not otherwise excluded under the other subclauses in
this clause (b)); provided further, however, that with respect to clauses (i), (ii), (iii),
(iv), (v), (vi) and (vii) above, any such effect shall be taken into account in determining whether
a Buyer Material Adverse Effect has occurred under this clause (b) to the extent it disproportionately affects Buyer and
its Subsidiaries, taken as a whole, relative to other participants in the industries or markets in which Buyer and its Subsidiaries operate
(in which case only such disproportionate portion shall be taken into account).

“Cap”
has the meaning set forth in Section 10.3(b).
“Cash”
means the aggregate amount of cash, cash equivalents, marketable securities and instruments and deposits of the Companies and their Subsidiaries
as of the Determination Time, in each case determined in accordance with the Accounting Principles. For the avoidance of doubt, Cash
shall (a) be calculated net of issued but uncleared checks and drafts written or issued by any Company or any of its Subsidiaries;
provided, however, that Cash shall not be reduced by issued but uncleared checks and drafts written or issued to the extent such
amounts are reflected as a current liability in the determination of Indebtedness or Transaction Expenses, and (b) include checks,
drafts and wire transfers deposited or in transit for the account of any Company or any of its Subsidiaries, other deposits in transit
and other credits in-process issued or initiated by any Company or any of its Subsidiaries. For purposes of determining Cash, the Parties
shall convert any amounts stated in currency other than Dollars into Dollars at the rate of exchange in effect as of the Determination
Time as published on Oanda.com.
“Claim”
means any claim (including any cross-claim or counterclaim), cause of action, allegation, charge, complaint, demand, inquiry (other than

“Closing Adjustment
Amount” means an amount, which may be expressed as a positive or a negative number, equal to (a) the difference between
Cash and the Target Cash Amount, expressed as (i) a positive number, if and only to the extent that Cash is greater than the Target
Cash Amount or (ii) a negative number, if and only to the extent that Cash is less than the Target Cash Amount minus (b) the
Closing Date Indebtedness and minus (c) the Unpaid Transaction Expenses.
“Closing Cash Consideration”
means an amount in cash equal to: (a) if the Estimated Closing Adjustment Amount is a positive number, the Estimated Closing Adjustment
Amount and (b) if the Estimated Closing Adjustment Amount is a negative number, Zero Dollars ($0).

has the meaning set forth in Section 2.2.
 7 
“Closing Date Indebtedness”
means the aggregate amount of Indebtedness that is outstanding as of the Determination Time.
“Closing Share Consideration
Adjustment Amount” means a number of Acquirer Shares equal to (a) the absolute value of the Estimated Closing Adjustment
Amount divided by (b) the Per Share Price.
“Closing Share Consideration
Amount” means the number of Acquirer Shares equal to: (a) if the Estimated Closing Adjustment Amount is a positive number,
the Closing Share Consideration Base Amount; or (b) if the Estimated Closing Adjustment Amount is a negative number, (i) the
Closing Share Consideration Base Amount minus (ii) the Closing Share Consideration Adjustment Amount.
“Closing Share Consideration
Base Amount” means the number of Acquirer Shares equal to 19.75% of the issued and outstanding Acquirer Shares as of immediately
prior to the Closing (but excluding any Acquirer Shares issued in a Permitted Financing or, subject to the terms of this definition,
upon conversion of equity securities issued in a Permitted Financing for Acquirer Shares, (a) if such Permitted Financing occurs
after October 8, 2026, (b) to the extent and only up to five hundred million Dollars ($500,000,000) in gross proceeds to Buyer,
individually and in the aggregate with all such Permitted Financings when combined with the gross proceeds of any and all other Permitted
Financings that occur after October 8, 2026, and (c) where each Acquirer Share is valued (i) in the case of any Acquirer
Share that is issued in such Permitted Financing, at the per share price paid for such Acquirer Share and (ii) in the case of any
other security that is issued or sold in such Permitted Financing and that is convertible into any Acquirer Share, at the conversion
price per share for such Acquirer Share.

including the interpretations thereof, or any change after the date hereof in the interpretation or enforcement of any of the foregoing,
or any new Law and any tariffs or other Taxes imposed under Order or any actual or prospective change in any Law, or any change in general
legal, regulatory, trade or political conditions; (f) any formal statement by or on behalf of the U.S. White House; (g) any
strike, slowdown or work stoppage, or any sequester, shutdown, default or similar event by or involving a Governmental Authority; (h) any
bankruptcy, insolvency or other financial distress of any customer, supplier or other counterparty of a Company or its Subsidiaries;
(i) the taking of any action expressly required or expressly permitted to be taken by Seller or any of the Companies by this Agreement
or the Related Agreements; (j) the negotiation, entry into or public announcement of this Agreement or pendency of the transactions
contemplated by this Agreement, including (1) any claim, suit, action or proceeding in connection with the transactions contemplated
by this Agreement or (2) any action taken in connection with obtaining regulatory consents including the actions required to be
taken by Buyer pursuant to Section 6.6; (k) the breach of this Agreement or the Confidentiality Agreement by Buyer (or
an Affiliate of Buyer, if applicable) to the extent such breach has proximately resulted in the failure of any condition set forth in
Section 8.3 to be satisfied; (l) any losses of employees, customers, suppliers or other counterparties of any Company
or any Subsidiary of a Company as a result of the execution and delivery or performance of this Agreement or the Related Agreements or
the transactions contemplated hereby or thereby or the identity of Buyer or any communication regarding plans or intentions with respect

in connection with taking any action or omitting to take any action at the express request of, or with the express approval or deemed
approval pursuant to Section 6.2 from, Buyer; (n) any action required to be taken or omitted to be taken under Law;
(o) any failure by the Companies (or any of them) to meet any projections, estimates or budgets for any period prior to, on or after
the date of this Agreement (it being understood that, solely with respect to this clause (o), the facts, events or circumstances
giving rise to such failure may themselves constitute or be taken into account in determining whether a Material Adverse Effect has occurred
if not otherwise excluded under the other subclauses of this definition) and (p) any aircraft mishap, failure or defect, including
in respect of any component or system related to such aircraft, with respect to that certain eVTOL air taxi developed by Wisk and referred
to as “The Wisk Aero Generation 6”; provided further, however, that with respect to clauses (a), (b),
(c), (d), (e), (f) and (g) above, any such effect shall be taken into account in determining
whether a Material Adverse Effect has occurred to the extent it disproportionately affects the Companies, taken as a whole, relative
to other participants in the industries or markets in which the Companies (or any of them) operate (in which case only such disproportionate
portion shall be taken into account).

1.2            Interpretation.
(a)            The
table of contents and the headings of the Articles, Sections and subsections included in this Agreement and the various headings of the
Disclosure Schedule are for convenience only and shall not be deemed part of this Agreement or the Disclosure Schedule or be given any
effect in interpreting this Agreement, the Disclosure Schedule or any Exhibits or Schedule hereto. Unless the context otherwise requires,
references in this Agreement to: (i) Articles, Sections, Exhibits and Schedules shall be deemed references to Articles and Sections
of, and Exhibits and Schedules to, this Agreement; (ii) “clauses” or “sub-clauses” shall be deemed references
to separate clauses or sub-clauses of the Section in which the reference occurs; (iii) any Contract (including this Agreement)
or Law shall be deemed references to such Contract or Law, as amended, supplemented or modified from time to time in accordance with
its terms and the terms hereof, as applicable, and in effect as of the relevant time of determination; (iv) any Person shall be
deemed references to such Person’s successors and permitted assigns, and in the case of any Governmental Authority, to any Person(s) succeeding
to its functions and capacities; and (v) any statute or other Law of the United States or other jurisdiction (whether federal, state
or local) shall be deemed references to all rules and regulations promulgated thereunder. Underscored references to Articles, Sections,
Subsections, Exhibits or Schedules shall refer to those portions of this Agreement.
(b)            Unless
the context otherwise clearly indicates, each defined term used in this Agreement shall have a comparable meaning when used in its plural
or singular form. The words “including,” “includes” or “include” are to be read as listing non-exclusive
examples of the matters referred to, whether or not words such as “without limitation” or “but not limited to”
are used in each instance. The words such as “herein,” “hereinafter,” “hereof” and “hereunder”
that are used in this Agreement refer to this Agreement as a whole and not merely to a subdivision in which such words appear unless
the context otherwise requires.
(c)            Where
this Agreement states that a Party “shall,” “will” or “must” perform in some manner or otherwise
act or omit to act, it means that such Party is legally obligated to do so in accordance with this Agreement. Time is of the essence
of each and every covenant, agreement and obligation in this Agreement.
 29 
(d)            Any
Contract, document, list or other item shall be deemed to have been “provided” or “made available” to Buyer for
all purposes of this Agreement if such Contract, document, list or other item was made available to Buyer or any of its Representatives,
including by being posted in the Data Room or by delivery of any kind of physical or electronic copy thereof.
(e)            Whenever
the word “Dollars” or the “$” sign appear in this Agreement, they shall be construed to mean United States Dollars,
and all transactions under this Agreement shall be in United States Dollars.
(f)            All
time periods within or following which any payment is to be made or act is to be done shall be calculated by excluding the date on which
the period commences and including the date on which the period ends, and solely with respect to any payment to be made, by extending
the period to the first (1st) succeeding Business Day if the last day of the period is not a Business Day. Unless specifically stated
to the contrary, all references to “days” herein shall be deemed to refer to calendar days.
(g)            The
Parties acknowledge and agree that, to the extent the terms and provisions of this Agreement are in any way inconsistent with or in conflict
with any term, condition or provision of any other Contract, document or instrument contemplated hereby, this Agreement shall govern
and control.
(h)            This
Agreement has been fully negotiated by the Parties and shall not be construed by any Governmental Authority or other Person against either
Party by virtue of the fact that such Party was the drafting Party.
ARTICLE 2
Purchase and Sale
2.1            Purchase
and Sale of the Purchased Interests. Upon the terms and subject to the conditions set forth in this Agreement, at the Closing, Seller
shall sell, assign, transfer and convey (or cause to be sold, assigned, transferred and conveyed) to Buyer, and Buyer or its Affiliate
shall purchase, acquire and accept (or cause to be purchased, acquired and accepted) from Seller or its applicable Affiliate, all of
Seller’s or such Affiliate’s right, title and interest in and to all of the Purchased Interests owned by Seller or such Affiliate
free and clear of all Liens other than restrictions on transfer under applicable securities Law or Liens created by Buyer or any of its
Affiliates, in exchange for the consideration specified in Section 2.3 and the mutual covenants and agreements contained
herein.
2.2            Closing.
(a)            Subject
to the following sentence, the Closing shall take place at the offices of Mayer Brown LLP, 71 S. Wacker Drive, Chicago, IL 60606,
on the date that is five (5) Business Days after the satisfaction (or waiver thereof by the Party entitled to benefit therefrom)
of the conditions precedent set forth in Article 8 (excluding the conditions that by their nature can only be satisfied at
the Closing, but subject to the satisfaction of such conditions at the Closing or the waiver of such conditions by the Party or Parties
entitled to waive such conditions) or on such other date, and at such other time and place, as may be agreed in writing by Buyer and
Seller; provided, however, that the Closing may occur remotely by exchange of documents and signatures via email or other manner
as may be mutually agreed upon by Buyer and Seller. Except as otherwise set forth herein, all actions to be taken and all documents to
be executed and delivered by all Parties at the Closing will be deemed to have been taken and executed simultaneously and no actions
will be deemed to have been taken nor documents executed or delivered until all have been taken, executed and delivered. The date on
which the Closing occurs in accordance with the preceding sentence is referred to in this Agreement as the “Closing Date”.
Unless the Parties agree otherwise, and notwithstanding the actual occurrence of the Closing at any particular time on the Closing Date,
the Closing shall be deemed to occur and be effective as of 12:01 am (local time wherever applied) on the Closing Date.

2.3            Closing
Payments. The aggregate amounts to be paid and securities to be issued by Buyer under this Section 2.3, as adjusted after
the Closing pursuant to Section 2.4 hereof, is referred to herein as the “Aggregate Consideration”. At
the Closing, Buyer shall:
(a)            issue
and deliver to Seller, a number of Acquirer Shares equal to the Closing Share Consideration Amount, free and clear of all Liens, and
cause such Acquirer Shares to be registered in book entry form in the name of Seller, or in such nominees name as designated by Seller
in writing, on Buyer’s share register;
(b)            pay
to Seller, the Closing Cash Consideration, if any;
(c)            pay
to each Person set forth in the “funds flow memo” as a “Transaction Expense Payee” therein the amount, as set
forth in the “funds flow memo” of Estimated Unpaid Transaction Expenses payable to such Person; and
(d)            deliver
to Seller, the Warrants, duly executed by Buyer.
The payments set forth in Section 2.3(c) by
Buyer will be considered payments on behalf of the Companies and in respect of obligations and liabilities of the Companies, and the
payments set forth in Sections 2.3(b) and 2.3(c) shall be made in cash by wire transfer of immediately available
funds to such account or accounts as are indicated by Seller in a “funds flow memo” to be delivered to Buyer by or on behalf
of Seller no later than three (3) Business Days prior to the Closing Date.
 33 
2.4            Closing
Adjustment Amount.
(a)            At
least three (3) Business Days prior to the Closing Date, Seller shall deliver, or cause to be delivered, to Buyer a statement duly
executed on behalf of Seller by an authorized officer of Seller (the “Pre-Closing Statement”), setting forth its good
faith estimate of (i) Cash (the “Estimated Cash”) and, based thereon, the amount by which Cash is greater than
the Target Cash Amount, or the amount by which Cash is less than the Target Cash Amount, (ii) Closing Date Indebtedness (the “Estimated
Closing Date Indebtedness”), (iii) Unpaid Transaction Expenses (the “Estimated Unpaid Transaction Expenses”)
and (iv) based upon each of the foregoing clauses (i) through (iii), the Closing Adjustment Amount (the “Estimated
Closing Adjustment Amount”). Seller shall prepare the Pre-Closing Statement in accordance with the definitions in this Agreement
and the Accounting Principles. To the extent there is a conflict between the definitions in this Agreement and the Accounting Principles,
the definitions in this Agreement shall prevail. Seller shall consider in good faith any reasonable comments made by Buyer to the Pre-Closing
Statement prior to the Closing; provided, that, the failure of Seller to implement such comments made by Buyer shall not delay
or otherwise prevent the Closing.
(b)            As
soon as reasonably practicable, but not later than ninety (90) days after the Closing Date, Buyer shall deliver to Seller a statement
(the “Post-Closing Statement”) setting forth Buyer’s reasonably detailed calculations of the Closing Adjustment
Amount, including each of its components: Cash, and, based thereon, the amount by which Cash is greater than the Target Cash Amount,
or the amount by which Cash is less than the Target Cash Amount, Closing Date Indebtedness and Unpaid Transaction Expenses, in each case
as of the Determination Time. The Post-Closing Statement and its components shall be prepared in good faith in accordance with the definitions
in this Agreement and the Accounting Principles, and shall also include a reasonably detailed reconciliation of any differences between
the calculations set forth in the Pre-Closing Statement and the Post-Closing Statement, together with reasonably detailed supporting
materials used in the preparation of the Post-Closing Statement and the calculations included therein.
(c)            In
connection with the review of the Post-Closing Statement by Seller, Buyer shall provide Seller and their Representatives with reasonable
access to the books and records, personnel, facilities and Representatives of each Company (and its successors or assigns). Furthermore,
Seller shall have the right to review the work papers of Buyer and of its Representatives underlying or utilized in preparing the Post-Closing
Statement and the calculation of the Closing Adjustment Amount, subject to customary access letters and confidentiality undertakings.
(d)            Within
ninety (90) days after its receipt of the Post-Closing Statement, Seller shall inform Buyer in writing either (x) that the Post-Closing
Statement is acceptable or (y) of any objection to the Post-Closing Statement or any of the components thereof, setting forth in
reasonable detail the basis for such objection and the specific adjustment to amounts, determinations and calculations set forth on the
Post-Closing Statement that Seller reasonably believes should be made (an “Objection Notice”). The deadline for an
Objection Notice shall be extended in the case of any delay by Buyer in providing Seller access to the books and records, personnel,
facilities and Representatives of each Company (and its successors or assigns) pursuant to Section 2.4(c) for each day
that Seller is not furnished with such access. If an Objection Notice is timely delivered within such ninety (90) day period (as so extended),
Buyer and Seller shall negotiate in good faith to resolve each dispute raised therein (each, a “Disputed Item”). If
Buyer and Seller fail to resolve any Disputed Item within ninety (90) days after Seller delivers an Objection Notice, then Buyer and
Seller shall jointly engage the Accounting Firm to resolve any remaining Disputed Items (but only those) as soon as practicable thereafter
(but in any event, within ninety (90) days after engagement of the Accounting Firm or such longer period as the Accounting Firm may reasonably
require). Nothing herein shall require a Party to agree or disagree to any Disputed Item. The amounts, determinations and calculations
(or any component thereof) contained in the Post-Closing Statement shall become final, conclusive and binding on the Parties at the following
times:
(i)            in
the event that Buyer does not deliver a Post-Closing Statement to Seller pursuant to Section 2.4(b) within ninety (90)
days after the Closing Date, then on the next Business Day following the expiration of such period (in which case such amounts, determinations
and calculations (or any component thereof) shall be as set forth in the Pre-Closing Statement delivered pursuant to Section 2.4(a) and
shall be considered the as agreed Post-Closing Statement for purposes of this Agreement);
 34 
(ii)            in
the event that Seller has informed Buyer in writing that the Post-Closing Statement is acceptable pursuant to this Section 2.4(d),
the date on which Seller so informs Buyer (in which case such amounts, determinations and calculations (or any component thereof) shall
be as set forth in the Post-Closing Statement delivered pursuant to Section 2.4(b));
(iii)           in
the event that Seller does not deliver an Objection Notice to Buyer pursuant to this Section 2.4(d) within ninety (90)
days after the receipt of the Post-Closing Statement (subject to extension as provided in this Section 2.4(d)), on the next
Business Day following the expiration of such period (in which case such amounts, determinations and calculations (or any component thereof)
shall be as set forth in the Post-Closing Statement delivered pursuant to Section 2.4(b));
(iv)           in
the event that Seller has delivered an Objection Notice to Buyer pursuant to this Section 2.4(d), the date of an agreement
in writing by Buyer and Seller that such amounts, determinations and calculations (or any component thereof) that are the subject of
such Objection Notice, together with any modifications thereto agreed to by Buyer and Seller, are final, conclusive and binding (in which
case such amounts, determinations and calculations (or any component thereof) shall be as agreed upon by Buyer and Seller); and
(v)            in
the event that Buyer and Seller engage the Accounting Firm to resolve any remaining Disputed Items pursuant to this Section 2.4(d),
the date on which the Accounting Firm issues its written resolution of such Disputed Items (in which case such amounts, determinations
and calculations (or any component thereof) shall be as resolved by the Accounting Firm pursuant to this Section 2.4(d) with
respect to all Disputed Items submitted to the Accounting Firm, and shall otherwise be as set forth in the Post-Closing Statement delivered
pursuant to Section 2.4(b), together with any modifications thereto agreed to by Buyer and Seller).
The Post-Closing Statement as agreed (or deemed
to be agreed) shall be the “Final Post-Closing Statement” for purposes of this Agreement, and shall be final, conclusive
and binding on the Parties absent manifest error and shall be used for the adjustment of the Closing Adjustment Amount, if any, pursuant
to Section 2.4(g). The statements of Cash, Closing Date Indebtedness and Unpaid Transaction Expenses set forth in the Final
Post-Closing Statement shall be the “Final Cash,” “Final Closing Date Indebtedness” and “Final
Unpaid Transaction Expenses,” respectively, for purposes of this Agreement.
 35 
(e)            Each
of the Parties agrees that the Accounting Firm (i) shall act as an expert and not as an arbitrator; (ii) shall be bound by
the provisions of this Section 2.4; (iii) shall not assign a value to any Disputed Item greater than the greatest value
claimed for such Disputed Item or less than the smallest value for such Disputed Item claimed by either Buyer in the Post-Closing Statement
or Seller in an Objection Notice; (iv) shall limit its determination to each unresolved Disputed Item; and (v) shall make its
determination based solely on presentations by Buyer and Seller which are in accordance with the guidelines and procedures set forth
in this Agreement (i.e., not on the basis of independent review).
(f)            Solely
for purposes of this Section 2.4, Buyer and Seller shall (x) furnish to each other and to the Accounting Firm such work
papers and other documents and information relating to the Disputed Items as the Accounting Firm may require and that are available to
the Party (or its independent public accountants) from whom such documents or information are requested or (y) cause the Accounting
Firm to deliver its determination of the Disputed Items to Buyer and Seller in writing, together with a reasonable basis for its determination
of each Disputed Item. In no event shall either of such Parties engage in ex parte communications with the Accounting Firm with
respect to any Disputed Item until the Accounting Firm issues its final determination in accordance with this Section 2.4(f).
The fees and expenses of the Accounting Firm incurred pursuant to this Section 2.4(f) shall be allocated between Buyer,
on the one hand, and Seller, on the other hand, based upon the percentage which the portion of the total contested amount not awarded
to each Party bears to the total contested amount actually contested by such Party. By way of illustration and not limitation, if the
total amount of the Disputed Items submitted to the Accounting Firm is $[***], and the Accounting Firm determines that Seller has a valid
claim for $[***] of the $[***], Seller will bear sixty percent ([***]%) of the fees and expenses of the Accounting Firm (i.e., the portion
not awarded to Seller ($[***]) divided by the total contested amount ($[***])) and Buyer will bear the remaining forty percent
([***]%) (i.e., the portion not awarded to Buyer ($[***]) divided by the total contested amount ($[***])).
In
the event of any dispute regarding the allocation required by this Section 2.4(f), the Accounting Firm shall determine
the allocation of its fees and expenses as between Buyer and Seller in accordance with the allocation methodology set forth in this Section 2.4(f),
and such determination shall be final and binding on both Buyer and Seller. Except as otherwise set forth in this Section 2.4(f),
the fees and expenses of Seller and its Representatives incurred in connection with the Post-Closing Statement and any Disputed Items
shall be borne by Seller, and the fees and expenses of Buyer and its Representatives incurred in connection with the Post-Closing Statement
and any Disputed Items shall be borne by Buyer.
(g)            If
the Closing Adjustment Amount (as finally determined in the Final Post-Closing Statement pursuant to Section 2.4(d)):
(i)            is
less than the Estimated Closing Adjustment Amount, then promptly and in any event within three (3) Business Days after the Closing
Adjustment Amount is finally determined in the Final Post-Closing Statement, Seller shall or shall cause its designated Affiliates, to
repay to Buyer an amount in cash, by wire transfer of immediately available funds, equal to the difference between the Closing Adjustment
Amount (as finally determined in the Final Post-Closing Statement pursuant to Section 2.4(d)) and the Estimated Closing Adjustment
Amount.
 36 
(ii)            exceeds
the Estimated Closing Adjustment Amount, then promptly and in any event within three (3) Business Days after the Closing Adjustment
Amount is finally determined in the Final Post-Closing Statement, Buyer shall or shall cause its designated Affiliates, to repay to Seller
an amount in cash, by wire transfer of immediately available funds, equal to the difference between the Closing Adjustment Amount (as
finally determined in the Final Post-Closing Statement pursuant to Section 2.4(d)) and the Estimated Closing Adjustment Amount.
The amounts in the foregoing
Section 2.4(g) shall be exclusive of any fees and expenses owed to the Accounting Firm by any Party pursuant to Section 2.4(f).
(h)            The
Pre-Closing Statement, the Post-Closing Statement and the determinations and calculations contained therein will be prepared and calculated
in accordance with the definitions in this Agreement and the Accounting Principles. To the extent there is a conflict between the definitions
in this Agreement and the Accounting Principles, the definitions in this Agreement shall prevail.
(i)            This
Section 2.4 shall be the sole and exclusive remedy of the Parties with respect to the determination of the Closing Adjustment
Amount.
(j)            Any
payments made pursuant to this Section 2.4 shall be treated by the Parties as an adjustment to the Aggregate Consideration
for Tax purposes, unless otherwise required by Law.
2.5            Allocation
of Purchase Price.
(a)            No
later than three (3) Business Days before the Closing Date, Seller and Buyer shall agree on the allocation of the consideration
paid at Closing under Section 2.3 among the Companies. At least twenty (20) Business Days prior to Closing, Seller shall
provide Buyer with its proposal of the allocation of the Aggregate Consideration (as finally determined) among the Companies. Buyer shall
review and provide its comments no later than ten (10) Business Days prior to Closing. The Parties shall negotiate in good faith
to resolve any dispute. Any remaining dispute among Buyer and the Seller shall be resolved by the Accounting Firm in accordance with
Section 2.4. Any adjustments to the Aggregate Consideration under Section 2.4 shall be allocated among the Companies
on a consistent basis with the foregoing.
(b)            The
Aggregate Consideration (and any other relevant items) shall be further allocated among the assets of the applicable Companies that are
treated as disregarded as separate from their owner for income Tax purposes in a manner consistent with Section 1060 of the Code
and the Treasury Regulations issued thereunder and the methodology agreed in Section 2.5(a). Within 45 calendar days after
the Final Post-Closing Statement is finally determined pursuant to Section 2.4, Seller will send to Buyer an allocation schedule
prepared in a manner consistent with this Section 2.5 (the “Purchase Price Allocation”). Buyer shall have
thirty (30) days to review the determinations set forth in the Purchase Price Allocation. If Buyer disagrees with any determinations
set forth in the Purchase Price Allocation, Buyer shall deliver a written notice to Seller setting forth its objections. Unless Buyer
delivers such notice to Seller within the thirty (30) day review period, Buyer shall be deemed to have accepted the determinations set
forth in the Purchase Price Allocation. If Buyer delivers the notice to Seller within the thirty (30) day review period, Buyer and Seller
shall, during the thirty (30) days following such delivery or any mutually agreed extension thereof, use their commercially reasonable
efforts to reach agreement on the disputed determinations. Any remaining dispute among Buyer and the Seller shall be resolved by the
Accounting Firm in accordance with Section 2.4. The Parties will file all Tax Returns in a manner consistent with the Purchase
Price Allocation, as finally determined under this Section 2.5, and not take any inconsistent position for Tax purposes.
 37 
2.6            Withholding
Taxes. Buyer and its respective Affiliates and agents will be entitled to deduct and withhold (or cause to be deducted and withheld)
from the consideration otherwise payable under this Agreement to Seller or any other Person any Taxes which are required to be deducted
and withheld under applicable Law with respect to the transactions contemplated in this Agreement. Buyer shall give Seller at least five
(5) Business Days advance notice of any requirement for withholding and the basis thereof and reasonably cooperate to reduce or
eliminate such withholding to the extent permitted by applicable Tax law. To the extent that amounts are so deducted and withheld and
properly paid over to the appropriate Tax Authority, such deducted and withheld amounts will be treated for all purposes of this Agreement
as having been paid to the Person in respect of which such deduction and withholding was made.
2.7            Australian
Foreign Resident Capital Gains Withholding. Seller has provided to Buyer, prior to the date of this Agreement, a declaration (in
the Australian Taxation Office preferred form NAT 74879-06.2016), on behalf of BAHPL, that, as at the date of this Agreement and for
the period from the date of this Agreement until the Closing, BAHPL is an Australian resident for Australian income tax purposes (“Seller
CGT Declaration”). Buyer acknowledges and agrees that:
(a)            the
Seller CGT Declaration given on behalf of BAHPL under this Section 2.7 represents a declaration for the purposes of section
14-210(3) of Schedule 1 to the Australian Tax Act given by BAHPL to the Buyer;
(b)            Buyer
does not know that the declaration is false;
(c)            because
of the declaration, and if the Closing occurs after the date that is six months after the date of this Agreement, subject to the receipt
of a further Seller CGT Declaration provided before the Closing for the period covering from date of this Agreement until the Closing,
Buyer will not (i) withhold any amount under Subdivision 14-D of Schedule 1 to the Australian Tax Act from any consideration payable
by the Buyer to BAHPL (or Seller or any other Affiliate of Seller); or (ii) pay any amount under Subdivision 14-D of Schedule 1
to the Australian Tax Act to the Australian Commissioner of Taxation, in connection with any consideration payable by the Buyer to BAHPL
(or any other Affiliate of Seller);
(d)            where
there are any amendments to Subdivision 14-D of Schedule 1 to the Tax Act which impact the operation and effect of the declaration made
by BAHPL under this Section 2.7 following the date of this Agreement and prior to Closing, the Parties will: (i) consult
and corporate in good faith and on a reasonable basis; and (ii) take all actions (acting reasonably) that are necessary or desirable
which may include obtaining a declaration from or otherwise notifying the Australian Taxation Office in respect of the Agreement, in
each case to otherwise ensure that, no liability to withhold or make a payment under Subdivision 14-D of Schedule 1 to the Tax Act arises.

2.8            Deferred
Closing.
(a)            Notwithstanding
anything to the contrary herein, solely with respect to Insitu Emirates, if the actions reasonably necessary to consummate the transactions
contemplated at Closing with respect to Insitu Emirates shall not have been completed by the Closing, despite the use of reasonable best
efforts by the Parties to complete such actions, then the transfer by Seller or its Affiliates to Buyer of Insitu Emirates, shall occur
not on the Closing Date, but shall instead occur on the Insitu Emirates Deferred Transfer Date (such transfer, a “Insitu Emirates
Deferred Transfer). The Parties shall use their respective reasonable best efforts to ensure that the Insitu Emirates Deferred Transfer
occurs as soon as reasonably practicable after the Closing Date (the date of such Insitu Emirates Deferred Transfer, the “Insitu
Emirates Deferred Transfer Date”).
(b)            In
the event that the actions reasonably necessary to consummate the transactions contemplated at Closing with respect to Insitu Emirates
shall not have been completed by the Closing:
(i)            Notwithstanding
anything to the contrary in this Agreement, each of the Parties acknowledges and agrees that (A) any calculation of the Purchase
Price (or components thereof) shall be made as if Insitu Emirates had transferred at the Closing (and shall disregard the fact that Insitu
Emirates is transferring after the Closing); (B) there shall be no change in the amount paid or issued at the Closing pursuant to
Section 2.3 as a result of the Insitu Emirates Deferred Transfer and the portion of the amounts to be paid and securities
to be issued that are allocable to Insitu Emirates as agreed to by the Parties shall be deemed to have been paid and issued upon payment
of the amounts and issuance of the securities contemplated by Section 2.3 at the Closing by or on behalf of Buyer in accordance
with Section 2.3; (C) the Insitu Emirates Deferred Transfer shall not otherwise affect the transactions contemplated
by this Agreement and the Related Agreements, and none of the conditions to the Closing set forth in Article 8 shall apply
to Insitu Emirates and, accordingly, the Closing shall not be delayed as a result of the Insitu Emirates Deferred Transfer.
(ii)            Each
Party agrees and covenants that it shall not assert the failure of any representation or warranty to be true and correct, or the breach
of any covenant or agreement, or the failure of any condition in Article 8 to be satisfied, in respect of Insitu Emirates
or an Insitu Emirates Deferred Transfer, as a reason or cause for the delay of, or failure to consummate, the Closing.
(iii)           From
and after the Closing, and until the Insitu Emirates Deferred Transfer Date, Insitu Emirates (including all Cash generated with
respect thereto) will be held for Buyer’s (or its Designated Buyer Affiliate’s) benefit and account and will be managed and
operated by Seller and its Affiliates for Buyer’s (or its Designated Buyer Affiliate’s) benefit and account.
(iv)           Notwithstanding
that legal title to Insitu Emirates will not be transferred to Buyer on the Closing Date, from the Closing Date until the Insitu Emirates
Deferred Transfer Date (A) Buyer and its Affiliates shall obtain (without infringing upon the legal rights of any Third Party or
violating any applicable Law) the economic rights and benefits of the business of Insitu Emirates, and any such income or gain shall
be paid over to Buyer by Seller or its Affiliates promptly upon the written request of Buyer and (B) (1) Buyer and its Affiliates
shall bear any related Liability with respect to Insitu Emirates; (2) Buyer or its Designated Buyer Affiliate shall reimburse Seller
or its Affiliates for all such Liabilities promptly upon the written request of Seller, in each case, which shall include reasonable
supporting documentation and (3) Buyer and its Affiliates shall indemnify and hold Seller and its Affiliates harmless from any Liabilities
with respect to Insitu Emirates.

filing such certifications. For purposes of this Section 5.6(d), “principal executive officer” and “principal
financial officer” have the meanings given to such terms in the Sarbanes-Oxley Act. Neither Buyer nor any of its Subsidiaries has
outstanding, or has arranged any outstanding, “extensions of credit” to directors or executive officers within the meaning
of Section 402 of the Sarbanes-Oxley Act.
(e)           Buyer
has established and maintains disclosure controls and procedures and internal control over financial reporting (as such terms are defined
in paragraphs (e) and (f), respectively, of Rule 13a-15 under the Exchange Act) as required by Rule 13a-15 under the Exchange
Act. Buyer’s disclosure controls and procedures are reasonably designed to ensure that all material information required to be
disclosed by Buyer in the reports that it files or furnishes under the Exchange Act is recorded, processed, summarized and reported within
the time periods specified in the rules and forms of the SEC, and that all such material information is accumulated and communicated
to Buyer’s management as appropriate to allow timely decisions regarding required disclosure and to make the certifications required
pursuant to Sections 302 and 906 of the Sarbanes-Oxley Act.
(f)           The
internal controls over financial reporting of Buyer and its Subsidiaries provide reasonable assurance regarding the reliability of the
financial reporting of Buyer and its Subsidiaries and the preparation of the financial statements of Buyer and its Subsidiaries for external
purposes in accordance with GAAP.
(g)           Buyer
has disclosed, based on its most recent evaluation prior to the date hereof, to Buyer’s auditors and the audit committee of Buyer
(i) all significant deficiencies and material weaknesses in the design or operation of internal controls over financial reporting

controls of Buyer or its Subsidiaries. Buyer has made available to Seller all such disclosures made by management to Buyer’s auditors
and audit committee of its board of directors.

5.9           Solvency.
Immediately after giving effect to the Closing and the transactions contemplated by this Agreement and the Related Agreements, Buyer
and its Subsidiaries (including the Companies) will be Solvent. For purposes of this Section 5.9, “Solvent”
shall mean that, with respect to any Person and as of any date of determination, (a) the amount of the “present fair saleable
value” of the assets of such Person, will, as of such date, exceed the amount of all “liabilities of such Person, contingent
or otherwise,” as of such date, as such quoted terms are generally determined in accordance with applicable United States federal
Laws governing determinations of the insolvency of debtors, (b) the present fair saleable value of the assets of such Person will,
as of such date, be greater than the amount that will be required to pay the liability of such Person on its indebtedness as its indebtedness
becomes absolute and matured, (c) such Person will not have, as of such date, an unreasonably small amount of capital with which
to conduct its business, (d) such Person will be able to pay its indebtedness as it matures and (e) with respect to Buyer,
the Buyer Enterprise Value is greater than [***] as of the date hereof. For purposes of the foregoing definition only, “indebtedness”
means a liability in connection with another Person’s (i) right to payment, whether or not such a right is reduced to judgment,
liquidated, unliquidated, fixed, contingent, matured, unmatured, disputed, undisputed, legal, equitable, secured or unsecured or (ii) right
to any equitable remedy for breach of performance if such breach gives rise to a right of payment, whether or not such right to an equitable
remedy is reduced to judgment, fixed, contingent, matured, unmatured, disputed, undisputed, secured or unsecured. No transfer is being
made and no obligation is being incurred in connection with the transactions contemplated hereby or by the Related Agreements with the
intent to hinder, delay or defraud either present or future creditors of Buyer or its Affiliates.

against Buyer in accordance with its terms, subject only to the Enforceability Exceptions and free and clear of all Liens other than

ARTICLE 6
Covenants
6.1           Access
to Information and Facilities.
(a)           Access.
Subject to Section 6.4, the Confidentiality Agreement and applicable Law, from the date of this Agreement to the earlier
of the Closing Date or the date this Agreement is terminated, the Companies shall give Buyer and Buyer’s Representatives, upon
reasonable notice, reasonable access, conducted at Buyer’s expense and solely for the purpose of assisting Buyer in satisfying
the closing conditions set forth in Section 8.2 (and not for any other purpose), during normal business hours and under the
supervision of personnel of the Companies or Seller, as applicable, to (x) information regarding the Companies and their operations
(including access to the offices and facilities, and books and records of the Companies) that are in the possession or under the control
of the Companies (but solely to the extent such information is readily available in an existing form without any material interference
with the business or operations of, or cost to, Seller or the Companies), subject to the terms hereof and (y) shall make the officers
and employees of the Companies available to Buyer and its Representatives as Buyer and its Representatives shall from time to time reasonably
request, in the case of the immediately foregoing clauses (x) and (y), to the extent that such access and disclosure
would not obligate Seller or the Companies to take any actions that would unreasonably disrupt the normal course of their businesses;
provided, however, that (i) all requests for access shall be directed to Christopher Fetscher and Joshua Crawford (each a
member of the Corporate Development group of Seller), or such other designee as Seller may appoint in writing from time to time and which
writing expressly references the appointment of such appointee pursuant to this Section 6.1(a) (each, a “Designated
Contact”) unless otherwise specified by Seller in writing and in no event shall there be any communications (including through
phone, email, social media, etc.) with any officers of the Companies or Business Employees until a Designated Contact has agreed
and arranged such communications; (ii) the auditors and accountants of Seller and the Companies shall not be obligated to make any
work papers available to any Person except in accordance with such auditors’ and accountants’ normal disclosure procedures
and then only after such Person has signed a customary agreement relating to such access in form and substance reasonably acceptable
to such auditors or accountants; (iii) Buyer shall not sample or analyze any soil, groundwater, other environmental media, building
or process material or wastestream in respect of the Companies, their facilities, their property or their operations; and (iv) Buyer
shall comply, and shall cause its Representatives to comply, with all safety, health and security rules applicable to any offices
or facilities being visited (including rules to comply with Public Health Measures) and all applicable Laws, including those regarding
the exchange of competitively sensitive information; provided, further, that nothing herein shall require Seller or the Companies
to provide access or to disclose any information to Buyer if such access or disclosure (A) would, in the reasonable judgment of
Seller, cause competitive harm to Seller or the Companies if the transactions contemplated by this Agreement are not consummated; (B) would
be in violation of Law (including any Antitrust Laws and those regarding the exchange of competitively sensitive information) applicable
to Seller or the Companies (or any of them) or the terms of any Contract to which Seller or Company is party; (C) breach any of
the Companies’, Seller’s or any of their respective Affiliates’ obligations to keep confidential or prevent access
to by reason of any Contract with a third party; (D) would compromise any attorney-client or an attorney work-product privilege
or would result in the waiver of any applicable attorney-client privilege; (E) is or may be, in the reasonable judgement of Seller,
requested in furtherance of, or preparation for, an Action against or involving Seller or any of their Affiliates (including prior to
the Closing, the Companies); (F) is related to any consent, approval, authorization, clearance from, or filing, notification or
registration with, any Governmental Authority set forth in Section 6.6 (which shall be governed by such Section 6.6);
or (G) relates to the sales process with respect to the transactions contemplated by this Agreement, including any correspondence,

any other Person (including any presentations or other materials prepared by or for Seller or the Companies or their respective Representatives,
whether in connection with a specific meeting, or otherwise relating to such subject matter). Other than as arranged through a Designated
Contact, Buyer is not authorized to and shall not (and shall cause its Associated Persons not to) contact (including through phone,
email, social media, etc.) any officer, director, employee, franchisee, customer, Governmental Authority, vendor, supplier, distributor,
lender, or other material business relation of Seller or the Companies prior to the Closing. The Companies shall have the right to have
a Representative present at all times during any inspections, interviews, and examinations conducted by Buyer. If Buyer or any of its
Representatives discovers any breach of or inaccuracy in any representation or warranty contained in this Agreement, or any error in

to, treat and hold strictly confidential any information provided or obtained pursuant to this Section 6.1(a) in accordance
with the Confidentiality Agreement.

(a)           Subject

obligations to take actions shall be governed by Section 6.6; provided, further that nothing in this Section 6.5(a) shall
require any Party to waive any of its conditions to the Closing. The “reasonable best efforts” of Seller as referenced in
such sections shall not require Seller, the Companies, or their respective Representatives to expend any amount of money or provide any
other consideration to (A) remedy any breach of a representation or warranty hereunder, (B) obtain any consent required for
consummation of the transactions contemplated hereby or (C) provide, assist or facilitate any financing arrangement; provided,
however, that if Seller, the Companies, or their Representatives elect to remedy any breach of a representation or warranty hereunder,
Seller shall not be deemed to be in breach of such representation or warranty, or in violation of any covenant, for purposes of determining
Buyer’s obligations to consummate the transactions contemplated hereby.

Contracts (other than any Government Contract) required in connection with the transactions contemplated hereby and by the Related Agreements;

any money (other than de minimis amounts) or provide any other consideration or commence, defend or participate in any litigation or
offer or grant any accommodation (financial or otherwise) to any Person.

(f)            In
furtherance and not in limitation of the covenants of the Parties contained in Section 6.5 and this Section 6.6,
in the event that any Governmental Authority requires any amendment, variation or modification to be made to this Agreement or any Related
Agreement, as a condition to such Governmental Authority approving Buyer as a suitable purchaser of the Purchased Interests or the businesses
conducted by the Companies and their Subsidiaries and/or confirming that the transactions contemplated by this Agreement will constitute
a divestiture of the Companies and their Subsidiaries or the businesses conducted by the Companies and their Subsidiaries, the Parties
agree that where such amendment, variation or modification would not reasonably be considered to (i) materially expand the nature
or scope of the transactions contemplated by this Agreement or (ii) constitute a Regulatory Constraint, the Parties shall use reasonable
best efforts to enter into such documents as are necessary to give effect to reflect such amendment, variation or modification, and,
in all other circumstances, the Parties will negotiate in good faith and use their respective reasonable efforts to accommodate and agree
to any such amendment, variation or modification and enter into any documents required to reflect such amendment, variation or modification
and in any event, no such amendment, variation or modification shall be implemented without the prior written consent of each Party.
(g)           In
respect of the DCSA Commitment, Buyer shall, at its sole cost and expense, be solely responsible for all aspects of the DCSA process
relating to the transactions contemplated hereby (the “DCSA Process”), including (i) as soon as is practicable
following the date of the agreement, any notification of a changed condition or change in ownership (including any updated Certificate
Pertaining to Foreign Interests (SF 328)) with respect to the relevant Company in accordance with the NISPOM, (ii) the preparation,
negotiation and submission to DCSA’s Risk Mitigation Unit of any foreign ownership, control, or influence mitigation plan (the
“FOCI Mitigation Plan”), together with any Electronic Communications Plan, Technology Control Plan, Visitation Policy
or other similar operational measure or compliance protocol required to implement the FOCI Mitigation Plan, and (iii) the strategy,
timing, form, content and all positions taken with DCSA in connection with the foregoing, in each case subject to Seller’s rights
in Section 6.6(b). Buyer shall use reasonable best efforts to obtain the DCSA Commitment as promptly as practicable following
the date hereof, and shall provide DCSA with any additional or supplemental information requested by DCSA as promptly as possible. Neither
Seller nor any of its Affiliates shall have any Liability under this Agreement for the failure to obtain the DCSA Commitment or for any
delay in, or condition imposed by DCSA in connection with, the DCSA Process, provided however, Seller shall, and shall cause the
relevant Company to, provide reasonable cooperation to Buyer and furnish information reasonably requested by Buyer in connection with
the DCSA Process, in each case at Buyer’s sole cost and expense.
(h)           Buyer
shall not, and shall cause its Affiliates not to, acquire or agree to acquire by merging or consolidating with, or by purchasing a substantial
portion of the assets of or equity in, or by any other manner, any Person or portion thereof, or otherwise acquire or agree to acquire
any assets, if entering into a definitive agreement relating to, or the consummation of, such acquisition, merger, consolidation or other
transaction would reasonably be expected to (i) impose any material delay in obtaining, or increase the risk of not obtaining, any
authorizations, consents, orders, declarations or approvals of any Governmental Authority necessary to consummate the transactions contemplated
hereby or the expiration or termination of any applicable waiting period, including under applicable Antitrust Laws, or as required to
obtain or make any Required Regulatory Approval, (ii) materially increase the risk of any Governmental Authority entering an order
prohibiting the consummation of the transactions contemplated hereby or (iii) materially delay the consummation of the transactions
contemplated hereby.

6.7           Termination
of Intercompany Commitments. Except as expressly set forth in (a) Schedule 6.7 of the Disclosure Schedule and (b) this
Agreement or in a Related Agreement, including Section 6.9 of this Agreement, all of the agreements and commitments (whether
written or oral), including all outstanding receivables, payables and other intercompany balances, between any Company, on the one hand,
and Seller or any of Seller’s Affiliates (other than the Companies), on the other hand, and all products and services provided
to the Companies by Seller or any of Seller’s Affiliates (other than the Companies), shall automatically terminate as of the Closing,
in each case without any further action or liability on the part of the parties thereto. The provision of any services by Seller or any
of Seller’s Affiliates (other than the Companies) to the Companies from and after the Closing will be for the convenience, and
at the expense, of Buyer only and will be furnished without any liability on the part of Seller or any of their Affiliates with respect
thereto.
6.8           Transfer
of Permits. Prior to the Closing, Seller shall use commercially reasonable efforts to transfer or cause to be transferred to the
Companies without any consideration all Permits that are listed on Schedule 6.8 of the Disclosure Schedule (if any), which are
held in the name of Seller or any of their Affiliates (other than the Companies) on behalf of the Companies and used in connection with
the operations of the Companies.
6.9           Release
of Guarantees. Seller and Buyer agree to cooperate and use their respective commercially reasonable efforts to promptly obtain the
release of Seller and its Affiliates that are a party to or otherwise have Liability with respect to (and to cause Buyer or its Subsidiaries
to be substituted for Seller and its Affiliates with respect to) any guarantees (whether parent, bank or other guarantees), performance
bonds, bid bonds, credit support agreements, letters of credit, offset obligations, obligations related to any Leased Real Property,
contingent liability obligations (including indemnification obligations) and other similar agreements or commitments, including those
set forth on Schedule 6.9 of the Disclosure Schedule (the “Seller Guarantees”) on or prior to the Closing,
in each case solely to the extent related to the Companies or any of their business, operations, assets or liabilities. Neither Seller
nor any of its Affiliates shall be required to incur any Liability in connection with obtaining the release of Seller or its Affiliates
with respect to any Seller Guarantees. In the event any of the Seller Guarantees are not released prior to or at the Closing, Buyer shall
(a) no later than at the Closing, obtain a letter of credit or surety insurance issued by a financial institution and on terms and
conditions reasonably acceptable to Seller, on behalf of Buyer or one of its Affiliates in favor of Seller or its Affiliates for the
value of each such Seller Guarantee, and (b) indemnify and hold harmless Seller and its Affiliates that are a party to or otherwise
have Liability, with respect to each such Seller Guarantee for any and all payments required to be made under such Seller Guarantee until
such Seller Guarantee is released, including, to the extent related to any Leased Real Property or the occupancy thereof, for any on-going
payments owed and other Liabilities incurred from and after the Closing; provided, that Buyer shall have no obligation to indemnify
Seller or any of its Affiliates under this Section 6.9 for any payments or Liabilities to the extent arising out of any gross
negligence, fraud or willful misconduct of Seller or any of its Affiliates existing or arising on or prior to the Closing; and provided,
further, that Buyer’s aggregate indemnification obligation with respect to each Seller Guarantee shall not exceed the value
of such Seller Guarantee and shall be reduced by any insurance proceeds or other third-party recoveries actually received by Seller or
its Affiliates in respect thereof (net of costs and expenses of pursuing such proceeds or recoveries). The foregoing indemnity will survive
the Closing with respect to each Seller Guarantee until such Seller Guarantee has been released, terminated or has expired in accordance
with its terms. Notwithstanding anything to the contrary contained in this Section 6.9, none of Seller nor any of its Affiliates
shall have any obligation to keep any Seller Guarantee in place for any renewal, extension, modification, supplement, amendment or rearrangement
of the terms of any or all of the obligations or liabilities relating to the Seller Guarantee. Buyer shall not, and shall not permit
any of its Affiliates (including the Companies after the Closing) to, renew or extend the term of or increase the obligations of the
Companies or their Affiliates under any letter of credit, bank guarantee or other Contract, or otherwise increase the Liabilities or
obligations of the Companies or their Affiliates for which Seller or any of its Affiliates have any performance or other obligations
under any Seller Guarantees, in each case except to the extent Buyer is able to, simultaneously or prior to such renewal, extension or
increase, obtain a release of Seller or its applicable Affiliate with respect to the applicable Seller Guarantee.

6.11         Enterprise-Wide
Contracts. Buyer acknowledges and agrees that the Enterprise-Wide Contracts set forth in Schedule 6.11(a) of the Disclosure
Schedule relate to both the business of the Companies and the business of Seller (or its Affiliates). Except as set forth on Schedule
6.11(b) of the Disclosure Schedule, all Enterprise-Wide Contracts shall be retained by Seller. From and after the Closing, none
of Buyer, the Companies or any of their respective Affiliates shall have any right, title, interest, preference, or remedy in respect
of an Enterprise-Wide Contract or shared service, and shall not be entitled to any service or product or to otherwise use or avail itself
of any Enterprise-Wide Contract or other shared service.
6.12         Exclusivity.
From the date of this Agreement until the Closing, none of Seller or any Company shall, directly or indirectly, (a) solicit, encourage
others to solicit, seek, facilitate or accept any inquiry, expression of interest, proposal or offer that constitutes, or could reasonably
be expected to lead to, an Alternative Transaction, (b) agree to, accept, recommend or endorse any Alternative Transaction or (c) disclose
or afford access to any person any nonpublic information relating to the Companies or any of their Subsidiaries in connection with, or
enter into, participate in, maintain or continue any discussions or negotiations regarding, any inquiry, expression of interest, proposal
or offer that constitutes, or could reasonably be expected to lead to, an Alternative Transaction; provided that, in the event
Seller or any Company or any of the Companies’ Subsidiaries or any of their Representatives receives an unsolicited communication
or other contact from a Person (other than Buyer and its Representatives) with respect to an Alternative Transaction, Seller or such
Representative may inform such Person of the restrictions set forth in this Section 6.12, without more, and such response
will not be deemed to be a violation of the restrictions set forth in this Section 6.12 so long as the Company continues
to comply with the other restrictions set forth in this Section 6.12. Seller shall promptly notify Buyer (without more) if
it or any of its Representatives receives any requests for information, proposals, inquiries or other contact from any Person relating
to an Alternative Transaction. Seller shall be responsible for the conduct of its Representatives or other Persons acting on its behalf
or for its benefit that is inconsistent with this Section 6.12.
