6.13          401(k) Plans.
Effective as of the day immediately preceding the Closing Date, contingent upon the Closing, Seller and the Companies will terminate
all Company Employee Plans that are intended to include a Section 401(k) arrangement and that are sponsored or maintained by
the Companies (each, a “Company 401(k) Plan”) (unless Buyer provides written notice to Seller no later than ten
(10) Business Days prior to the Closing Date that such Company 401(k) Plans should not be terminated), in accordance with the
terms of each such Company 401(k) Plan and applicable Law. Seller and the Companies will provide Buyer with evidence that such Company
401(k) Plans have been terminated (effective no later than the day immediately preceding the Closing Date). The form and substance
of such resolutions will be subject to review and reasonable comment by Buyer, which comments shall be considered in good faith. Effective
as of the Closing, Buyer shall allow each Continuing Employee who is a participant in a Company 401(k) Plan on the Closing Date
to (a) participate, effective as soon as administratively practicable following the Closing Date, in a 401(k) plan sponsored
by Buyer or an Affiliate thereof (the “Buyer 401(k) Plan”) and (b) be eligible to make direct rollover contributions
from a Company 401(k) Plan to the Buyer 401(k) Plan of “eligible rollover distributions” within the meaning of
Section 401(a)(31) of the Code, including the in-kind direct rollover of any participant loans. For the avoidance of doubt, if necessary,
Buyer shall amend Buyer’s 401(k) Plan to permit the in-kind direct rollover of participant loans from a Company 401(k) Plan.

Agreement. Buyer agree to take, or cause to be taken, all actions, and do, or cause to be done, all things necessary, proper and

to Seller in its sole discretion, consenting to the assumption of the Offset Obligation (effective as of Closing, in the event that such
agreement or assurance is obtained prior to Closing) and all Liabilities thereunder by Buyer or its applicable Affiliate, including a
full and unconditional release of Seller and its Affiliates from all Liability related to the Offset Obligation and/or the Offset Project
(the “Offset Release”). Notwithstanding anything to the contrary in this Agreement, this Agreement shall not constitute
an agreement to assign or transfer the Offset Obligation if (a) an assignment or transfer thereof, without the consent of the Offset
Authority or any other Governmental Authority, would constitute a breach or violation thereof or impose any Liability on Seller or any
of its Affiliates, or result in the termination thereof or the creation of any Lien on any of the assets of Seller or any such Affiliate,
and (b) such consent is not obtained at or prior to the Closing. In the event that the Offset Release has not been obtained prior
to Closing, following the Closing and until such time as the Offset Release has been obtained, Buyer shall (i) continue to pursue
the Offset Release in accordance with the terms of this Section 6.16 and (ii) perform, or cause its Affiliate to perform,
in each case at its sole cost and expense, the Offset Project and otherwise to satisfy all obligations of Seller and its Affiliates in
respect of the Offset Obligation, in each case in accordance with the terms of the Offset Agreement.

NYSE Confirmation. In the event that the NYSE Confirmation is not received in form and substance satisfactory to Seller prior to the
Closing, each Party shall execute and deliver the Forward Equity Purchase Agreement to the other Party at the Closing.

ARTICLE 7
Post-Closing Covenants
7.1           Preservation
of Records; Post-Closing Access and Cooperation.
(a)            For
a period of six (6) years after the Closing Date, Buyer shall use commercially reasonable efforts to, and shall cause its respective
Affiliates (including, after the Closing, the Companies and their Subsidiaries) to use commercially reasonable efforts to preserve and
retain all corporate, accounting, legal, auditing, human resources and other books and records (including any documents relating to any
Governmental Authority or non-Governmental Authority claims, actions, suits, proceedings or investigations) relating to the conduct of
the business and operations of the Companies prior to the Closing Date or in the case of books and records as to which applicable Law
requires a longer period of retention, such longer period) that are in the possession of Buyer, the Companies or their respective Affiliates.
On and after the end of such period, Buyer shall use commercially reasonable efforts to, and shall cause its Affiliates to use commercially
reasonable efforts to, provide Seller with at least five (5) Business Days’ prior written notice before destroying, altering
or otherwise disposing of any such books and records, during which period Seller may elect to take possession, at Seller’s sole
cost and expense, of such books and records. Notwithstanding the foregoing, Buyer shall use commercially reasonable efforts to cause
the Companies and their Subsidiaries to retain the books and records with respect to Tax matters or other matters to the extent required
by applicable Law that are in the possession of the Companies at the Closing until the expiration of the applicable statute of limitations,
including any extensions thereof, and in compliance with applicable Law.
 99 
(b)           For
a period of six (6) years after the Closing, Buyer shall use commercially reasonable efforts, and shall cause its respective Affiliates
(including, after the Closing, the Companies and their Subsidiaries) to use commercially reasonable efforts to, (x) afford to Seller
and its Representatives reasonable access during normal business hours (and in a manner that does not unreasonably interfere with the
operations of Buyer, the Companies or their Affiliates) to: (i) the books and records (including for the purpose of examining and
copying) relating to the conduct of the business and operations of the Companies prior to the Closing Date that are in the possession
or control of Buyer, the Companies or their Affiliates; and (ii) personnel (including members of the accounting function) of Buyer,
the Companies and their Affiliates for purposes of better understanding such books and records and any questions of Seller or its Affiliates
related thereto, in each case, to the extent and for a purpose reasonably requested by Seller, including (A) for the purpose of
preparing any Tax Returns or financial statements or (B) as may be necessary or required pursuant to applicable Law or any audit
request, subpoena or other investigative demand by any Governmental Authority or for any Actions, other than against Buyer or its Affiliates
(including), and (y) at Seller’s expense, to provide any and all information (including financial statements, historical and
pro forma financial data, trial balances, supporting schedules and other similar information of the type reasonably requested by Seller)
with respect to the Companies as is reasonably necessary for Seller to prepare financial statements, adhere to applicable Law or reporting
requirements (including any SEC reporting requirements on a quarterly or annual basis), or generate other financial information reasonably
requested by Seller following the Closing in connection with Seller’s SEC reporting requirements.
(c)            Upon
the reasonable request of Buyer or Seller, each Party will on and after the Closing Date execute and deliver to the other Parties such
other documents, assignments and other instruments as may be reasonably required to effectuate completely the transactions contemplated
by this Agreement and the Related Agreements, and to effect and evidence the provisions of this Agreement and the Related Agreements
and the transactions contemplated hereby and thereby; provided, however, that such documents, assignments and other instruments
shall not increase the liability or obligations of the executing party beyond those which are contemplated by this Agreement and the
Related Agreements.

and Benefits.
(a)            As
of the date hereof, Buyer has no present intention of terminating the employment of any Business Employees who are employed or engaged
by or providing services to the Companies or any of their Subsidiaries as of the Closing Date. Each of Buyer and Seller agrees to, and
Seller agrees to cause the Companies to, use commercially reasonable efforts to cooperate with and to provide information to each other
as necessary or appropriate to comply with or satisfy any requirement or custom to consult with or provide information to, with respect
to any of the transactions contemplated by this Agreement, any employee or labor organization representing or related to Business Employees.
 100 
(b)            In
the event that the transactions contemplated by this Agreement constitute a relevant transfer for the purposes of the Transfer Regulations
and, as a consequence the Transfer Regulations apply to the UK Seller Employees and/or the Dutch Seller Employee, the contract of employment
or engagement of each such UK Seller Employees and/or the Dutch Seller Employee shall on the Closing Date transfer to the Buyer, an Affiliate
of the Buyer and/or to a Third Party Employer pursuant to the Transfer Regulations (except in so far as such contract relates to (i) any
benefits for old age, invalidity or survivors provided under any occupational pension scheme; (ii) any work location immediately
prior to Closing; and (iii) any UK Seller Employee or Dutch Seller Employee who objects to the transfer of their contract of employment
under the Transfer Regulations) and with effect from the Closing Date, such contract of employment or engagement shall be as if originally
made between Buyer, the relevant Affiliate of Buyer, or the relevant Third Party Employer (as applicable) and each UK Seller Employee
and the Dutch Seller Employee, and all the relevant Seller’s rights, powers, duties and liabilities under or in connection with
such contracts and employment relationships (except for any liabilities which may be and are excluded from transfer in accordance with
the Transfer Regulations) shall transfer to Buyer, the relevant Affiliate of Buyer and/or the relevant Third Party Employer(as applicable).
Each UK Seller Employee and the Dutch Seller Employee who transfers to Buyer, an Affiliate of Buyer or to a Third Party Employer pursuant
to the Transfer Regulations is referred to herein as a “Transferring Employee”. Each of Buyer and Seller agrees to,
and Seller agrees to cause the Companies to and Buyer agrees to cause any relevant Affiliate of Buyer and any Third Party Employer to,
reasonably cooperate with and to provide reasonable information to each other party so as to enable each party to comply with and/or
satisfy any requirement to consult with and/or provide information to, with respect to any of the transactions contemplated by this Agreement,
any UK Seller Employee or the Dutch Seller Employee or any appropriate representative of any of the UK Seller Employees or the Dutch
Seller Employee, and otherwise to comply with Buyer’s, any Affiliate’s of Buyer and/or any Third Party Employer’s obligations
under the Transfer Regulations. Neither Buyer nor Seller shall (and Buyer shall procure that any relevant Affiliate of Buyer and any
Third Party Employer shall not) take or cause to be taken any actions that would result in the employment or engagement of any of the
UK Seller Employees or the Dutch Seller Employee not transferring to Buyer, any Affiliate of Buyer or any Third Party Employer on the
Closing Date under applicable Law and/or which would cause any such party to be in breach of its obligations under applicable Law. In
the event that, for any reason, it is found or alleged that the Transfer Regulations will not operate to transfer the contract of employment
or engagement of any UK Seller Employee or the Dutch Seller Employee to Buyer, any Affiliate of Buyer or any Third Party Employer, the
provisions of Section 7.2(c) below will apply to such UK Seller Employee or the Dutch Seller Employee.
(c)            At
least 30 days prior to the Closing Date, Buyer shall, or shall procure that any Affiliate of Buyer or any Third Party Employer (as applicable),
make an offer of employment in writing to each Seller Employee set forth on Schedule 7.2(c) of the Disclosure Schedule, which
offers shall be effective so as to commence employment with Buyer, any Affiliate of Buyer and/or any Third Party Employer on the Closing
Date. Offers pursuant to this Section 7.2(c) shall, subject to Sections 7.2(g), 7.2(h) and 7.2(i),
(i) be on terms and conditions which are comparable in the aggregate to those which such Seller Employee enjoyed immediately prior
to the Closing Date; provided that where such offer includes (i) a change to such Seller Employee’s work location within
20 km from such Seller Employee’s work location prior to such offer or (ii) a change to working on a fully remote basis, such
offer shall be deemed to be comparable for the purposes of this Section 7.2(c); (ii) be sufficient to avoid statutory,
contractual, common Law or other severance obligations and (iii) otherwise comply in all respects with applicable Law (including
with respect to compensation and benefits) and with the terms required by this Section 7.2(c). For the avoidance of doubt,
this provision shall not require Buyer, any Affiliate of Buyer or any Third Party Employer to establish, or admit to participation, a
Seller Employee to a defined benefit pension arrangement. Each Seller Employee who accepts such offer of employment with Buyer, its Affiliates
or any Third Party Employer is referred to herein as an “Accepting Employee”.
 101 
(d)            Any
Seller Employee who objects to the transfer of their contract of employment under the Transfer Regulations under Section 7.2(b) or
does not accept an offer of employment made under Section 7.2(c) will remain an employee of Seller and ongoing employment,
including any termination of employment of such employee, will be at the cost of Seller. Buyer, the Affiliate of Buyer or any Third Party
Employer will bear no liabilities in relation to such employees unless Buyer, an Affiliate of Buyer or a Third Party Employer has failed
to comply with its obligations pursuant to Section 7.2(c) or additionally in the case of the UK Seller Employees or
the Dutch Seller Employee, the relevant individual has objected to the transfer due to Buyer, any Affiliate of Buyer or any Third Party
Employer having proposed a substantial change in working conditions to the material detriment of the relevant individual or otherwise
in breach of the Transfer Regulations or the terms of this Agreement, in which case Buyer’s obligations shall be dealt with in
accordance with Section 7.2(j).
(e)            Prior
to the Closing Date, Buyer and Seller shall use commercially reasonable efforts to coordinate the transition of the employment of each
Seller Employee set forth in Schedule 7.2(e) of the Disclosure Schedule in connection with the transactions contemplated
by this Agreement, which may include Seller transferring the employment of such Seller Employees to one of the Companies or a Subsidiary
of a Company. At least five (5) days prior to the Closing Date, Seller shall deliver to Buyer an updated copy of Schedule 7.2(e) of
the Disclosure Schedule to reflect any changes since the date hereof. Upon the transfer of employment of such Seller Employees to the
Companies or their Subsidiaries pursuant to this Section 7.2(e), such individuals shall be deemed “Company Employees”
for purposes of this Section 7.2 only. With respect to such Seller Employees, Buyer shall, or shall cause its Affiliates
or any Third Party Employer to, assume and pay to each Seller Employee an annual bonus for the performance period that includes the Closing
Date; provided, however, that (i) the Company shall have accrued for the pre-Closing component of the performance period,
and (ii) in any event, the bonus payments to such employees shall be no less than the amount of such accrual, with the accrual as
of the date of this Agreement being accrued in the ordinary course of business.
(f)            Schedule
7.2(f) of the Disclosure Schedule lists those U.S. based Company Employees who are seconded to Seller or one of its Affiliates
(the “Seconded Company Employees”), along with their employing entity, the entity they are seconded to and the country
location of each Seconded Company Employee’s secondment. At least one day prior to the Closing, the Companies and/or their Subsidiaries,
as applicable, shall terminate the Seconded Company Employees’ secondments with Seller and/or its Affiliates and, unless notified
by Buyer at least sixty (60) days prior to the Closing Date, shall repatriate such Seconded Company Employees. For the avoidance of doubt,
upon the termination of such secondments, the Seconded Company Employees shall remain Company Employees. At least sixty (60) days prior
to the Closing Date, Buyer shall identify in writing to Seller the name of the Affiliate of Buyer or Third Party Employer to which each
Seconded Company Employee will be seconded on or after the Closing Date, if applicable, and the date each such secondment shall begin.
Seller shall be solely responsible for, and shall pay, satisfy and discharge, any and all Liabilities arising out of or relating to the
termination of any secondment existing as of immediately prior to the Closing and/or the repatriation of any Seconded Company Employee
in connection therewith. Notwithstanding the foregoing, to the extent that Buyer has notified Seller pursuant to this Section 7.2(f) that
a Seconded Company Employee will commence a secondment with Buyer or one of its Affiliates or a Third Party Employer following the Closing,
Buyer shall be solely responsible for any Liabilities arising out of or relating to such post-Closing secondment and any subsequent termination
thereof.
 102 
(g)           For
a period of no less than two (2) years following the Closing Date, Buyer shall, or shall cause the Companies or their Subsidiaries,
any Affiliate of Buyer or any Third Party Employer, to provide to each Company Employee, each Transferring Employee and each Accepting
Employee (each, a “Continuing Employee” and collectively, the “Continuing Employees”) who is not
covered by a collective bargaining agreement (i) a base salary or wages that are not less than the base salary or wages provided
to such Continuing Employee immediately prior to the Closing, (ii) variable/incentive/bonus pay opportunities that are no less than
(including any value attributable to equity and equity-based compensation) those provided to such Continuing Employee immediately prior
to the Closing, (iii) severance benefits that are no less favorable than those that would have been provided to such Continuing
Employee immediately prior to the Closing, through statutory or contractual entitlements or severance set forth on Schedule 4.8(a) of
the Disclosure Schedule, (or, in the case of Seller Employees, those provided to similarly situated Company Employees immediately prior
to the Closing Date), and (iv) excluding any defined benefit or similar pension plans, other benefit plans and arrangements that
are no less favorable in the aggregate to those provided to such Continuing Employee immediately prior to the Closing Date (or, in the
case of Seller Employees, those provided to similarly situated Company Employees immediately prior to the Closing Date). For the avoidance
of doubt, with respect to each Continuing Employee, Buyer shall, or shall cause its Affiliates to, assume and pay to each Continuing
Employee an annual bonus for the performance period that includes the Closing Date; provided, however, that (i) the Company
shall have accrued for the pre-Closing component of the performance period, and (ii) in any event, the bonus payments to such employees
shall be no less than the amount of such accrual.
(h)           For
the avoidance of doubt, effective as of the Closing, and where required by applicable Law, for each Continuing Employee, Buyer, any Affiliate
of Buyer or Third Party Employer shall, and shall cause the Companies and their Subsidiaries to, recognize such Continuing Employee’s
accrued but unused leave and/or PTO benefits, as well as any accrued end of service gratuity, as of immediately prior to the Closing
Date, and to maintain and/or credit (where applicable) each such Continuing Employee with such accrual. Notwithstanding the foregoing,
to the extent that applicable Law requires any severance, termination, end of service gratuity, termination indemnity or similar payment
or benefit to be paid, accrued, funded or otherwise settled as a result of or in connection with the transfer of employment of any Continuing
Employee at or in connection with the Closing, Seller shall retain, and be solely responsible for, all Liabilities in respect thereof
for the period prior to the Closing. Buyer, any Affiliate of Buyer or any Third Party Employer, in accordance with and subject to applicable
Law, shall and shall cause the Companies and their Subsidiaries to permit each Continuing Employee to take accrued but unused leave and/or
PTO days recognized and credited by Buyer and/or the Companies or their Subsidiaries, as applicable. As of the Closing, where required
and subject to applicable Law, Buyer shall permit (and shall procure that each Affiliate of Buyer and each Third Party Employer shall
permit) each Continuing Employee to accrue additional leave and/or PTO days in accordance with the terms Buyer provides to similarly
situated employees of Buyer immediately prior to Closing, after giving pre-Closing service credit to each Continuing Employee as provided
in Section 7.2(i)(i)(A), or in accordance with the terms of their contract of employment, whichever is the greater.
 103 
(i)            To
the extent applicable with respect to employee benefit plans, programs and arrangements that are established or maintained by Buyer,
its Affiliates (including, for periods after the Closing, the Companies and/or their Subsidiaries) and any Third Party Employer for the
benefit of Continuing Employees (each, a “Buyer Plan”), Continuing Employees (and their eligible dependents) (i) shall
be given credit for their service with the Companies, Seller and their Affiliates, and any Seller Group Member (A) for all purposes,
including eligibility, vesting and benefit accrual (other than benefit accrual under any defined benefit pension plans), to the extent
such service was taken into account under a corresponding Benefit Plan or other benefit plan, program and arrangement immediately prior
to the Closing, and (B) for purposes of satisfying any waiting periods, evidence of insurability requirements, or the application
of any pre-existing condition limitations, and (ii) shall be given credit for amounts paid under a corresponding Benefit Plan or
other benefit plan, program and arrangement during the same period for purposes of applying deductibles, copayments and out-of-pocket
maximums as though such amounts had been paid in accordance with the terms and conditions of the Buyer Plans. Notwithstanding the foregoing
provisions of this Section 7.2(i), service and other amounts shall not be credited to Continuing Employees (or their eligible
dependents) to the extent the crediting of such service or other amounts would result in duplication of benefits. Except as expressly
contemplated by this Section 7.2, none of Buyer, its Affiliates or any Third Party Employer (including the Companies after
the Closing) shall assume any obligations or Liabilities under any Seller Benefit Plan.
(j)            On
and after the Closing, Buyer, any Affiliate of Buyer, any Third Party Employer, the Companies and/or their Affiliates shall be responsible
for any and all notices and Liabilities arising from any action on or after the Closing by Buyer, any Affiliate of Buyer, any Third Party
Employer, the Companies and/or their Affiliates (including breach of contract, defamation or retaliatory discharge) regarding the Continuing
Employees and the Transferring Employees, including any such Liability (i) under any Law that relates to employees, employee benefit
matters or labor matters, (ii) for dismissal, wrongful termination, unfair and/or constructive dismissal or termination, claims
for reinstatement or severance pay, termination pay, prior reasonable notice of termination or pay in lieu of reasonable prior notice
of termination, (iii) under or with respect to any benefit plan, program, collective bargaining agreement, Contract, policy, commitment
or arrangement of Buyer, the Companies and their Affiliates, and any Third Party Employer including with respect to severance or retention
plans, or to the extent such severance or retention plans provide payments or benefits with respect to any Continuing Employee or any
Transferring Employees, or (iv) for any failure of Buyer, any Affiliate of Buyer any Third Party Employer, the Companies or their
Affiliates to discharge their respective obligations under this Section 7.2. Buyer, its Affiliates and any Third Party Employer
shall also be responsible for any and all notices and Liabilities arising from any statement and/or act by Buyer its Affiliates and/or
any Third Party Employer prior to Closing (including but not limited to any measures proposed in relation to the working conditions or
otherwise of any of the UK Seller Employees or the Dutch Seller Employee) which entitles any of the UK Seller Employees or the Dutch
Seller Employee to resign from their employment or be treated as having been dismissed under the Transfer Regulations.

(k)            In
any termination, reduction of work hours, or layoff of any Company Employee by Buyer, any Affiliate of Buyer, any Third Party Employer
or the Companies or their Subsidiaries on or after the Closing, Buyer, such Affiliate of Buyer, such Third Party Employer and the Companies
and their Subsidiaries will comply fully (or cause their Affiliates to comply fully), if applicable, with the WARN Act and all other
applicable Laws requiring notice to employees. Buyer shall not, and shall cause its Affiliates, any Third Party Employer, the Companies

of employment, operating unit, Continuing Employees or employee of the Companies or their Subsidiaries without complying fully with the
requirements of the WARN Act or similar Laws. Buyer and the Companies will bear the cost of compliance with (or failure to comply with)
any such Laws.

(m)          Notwithstanding
the foregoing, this Section 7.2 shall not limit the obligation of Buyer, the Companies or any of their Affiliates or any
Third Party Employer to comply with Laws or to maintain any compensation arrangement or benefit plan in effect. No provision of this
Agreement shall be construed as a guarantee of continued employment of any Continuing Employee or any Transferring Employee for any specified
period following the Closing, and this Agreement shall not be construed so as to prohibit Buyer, the Companies or any of their Affiliates
or any Third Party Employer from having the right to terminate the employment of any Continuing Employee or any Transferring Employee;
provided, however, that any such termination is effected in accordance with Law. No provision of this Section 7.2
or any other provision shall create any third party beneficiary or other rights in any person, including any employee or former employee
(including any beneficiary or dependent thereof) of the Companies, Seller or of any of their Affiliates in respect of continued employment
(or resumption of employment), and no provision of this Section 7.2 shall create any such rights in any such individuals
in respect of any benefits that may be provided, directly or indirectly, under any Benefit Plan, or any plan or arrangement that may
be established by the Companies. Subject to applicable Law, unless otherwise provided in this Section 7.2, no provision of
this Agreement shall constitute a limitation on rights to amend, modify or terminate, either before or after Closing, any Benefit Plan.
7.3           Public
Announcements. The Parties may issue a joint press release at the Closing in the form attached hereto as Exhibit M (the
“Agreed Press Release”). Except for the Agreed Press Release, neither Party shall, and each Party shall cause its
Affiliates and their respective Representatives not to, issue any press release or otherwise make any public statements or disclosures
(including commenting on, characterizing, or releasing any details) with respect to this Agreement, including the terms hereof, and the
transactions contemplated hereby and by the Related Agreements, except (a) with the prior written consent of the other Party; (b) to
the extent expressly set forth on the face of the Agreed Press Release, (c) to the extent required by applicable Law or the rules of
any securities exchange on which a Party’s or a Party’s Affiliates securities are traded (in which case the Party issuing
such press release or making such public statement shall, if practicable in the circumstances, use commercially reasonable efforts to
allow the other Parties reasonable time to comment on such release or statement in advance of its issuance and will consider in good
faith the advice of such other Party or Parties with respect thereto); or (d) for announcements by Seller or the Companies from
time to time to their respective employees, customers, suppliers and other business relations and otherwise as they may reasonably determine
is necessary (including to comply with applicable Law, this Agreement and the transactions contemplated hereby and by the Related Agreements
or any other agreement to which they are party).

7.4           D&O
Indemnification.
(a)           Buyer
agrees that all rights to exculpation, indemnification and advancement of expenses now existing in favor of the current or former equity
holders, directors, officers or employees, as the case may be, of any Company (or directors or officers of any Company to the extent
serving as fiduciaries with respect to any Benefit Plan maintained by any of the Companies) as provided in any of the Companies’
Governing Documents or in any agreement that has been disclosed to Buyer in the Disclosure Schedule shall survive the Closing and shall
continue at and after the Closing in full force and effect. For six (6) years from and after the Closing Date, Buyer shall cause
the Companies, to indemnify and hold harmless all of the past and present directors and officers (and any other person entitled to indemnification
from the Company on a similar basis as set forth on Schedule 7.4(a) of the Disclosure Schedule) of any Company to the same
extent such Persons are indemnified by any Company or Seller as of the date hereof pursuant to the Governing Documents of such Company
or any agreement between any Company and such equity holder, officer or director that have been disclosed to Buyer in the Disclosure
Schedule, for acts or omissions occurring at or prior to the Closing Date, and Buyer shall not permit any Company to amend, repeal or
modify any provision in such Company’s Governing Documents, or any agreement with such officer or director relating to the exculpation
or indemnification of former officers and directors as in effect immediately prior to the Closing Date.
(b)            At
or prior to the Closing, the Companies shall obtain a prepaid, non-cancelable “tail” directors’ and officers’
liability insurance policy, with a claims reporting period of six (6) years from the Closing Date (the “D&O Tail Policy”)
covering those Persons who are currently covered by any Company’s or Seller’s directors’ and officers’ liability
insurance policies on terms not less favorable than such existing insurance coverage with respect to acts or omissions occurring prior
to Closing; provided, however, that in the event that any claim is brought under such director’s and officer’s liability
insurance policies, such policies shall be maintained until final disposition of such claim.
(c)            Notwithstanding
anything contained in this Agreement to the contrary, this Section 7.4 shall survive the Closing until the sixth (6th) anniversary
of the Closing Date. In the event that Buyer or any Company or any of their respective successors or assigns (i) consolidates with
or merges into any other Person, or (ii) transfers all or substantially all of its properties or assets to any Person, then, and
in each case, Buyer shall cause the successors and assigns of Buyer or such Company, as the case may be, to expressly assume and be bound
by the obligations set forth in this Section 7.4.
(d)           The
obligations of Buyer and each Company under this Section 7.4 shall not be terminated or modified in such a manner as to adversely
affect any D&O Indemnitee to whom this Section 7.4 applies without the written consent of such affected D&O Indemnitee.

7.5           Waivers
and Releases.
(a)           EFFECTIVE
AS OF THE CLOSING, SELLER DOES FOR ITSELF AND ITS AFFILIATES, SUCCESSORS AND ASSIGNS, IF ANY, AND THEIR RESPECTIVE HEIRS, BENEFICIARIES,
SUCCESSORS AND ASSIGNS (EACH, A “SELLER RELEASING PARTY”), VOLUNTARILY, IRREVOCABLY, FINALLY AND UNCONDITIONALLY
FOREVER RELEASES, WAIVES AND ABSOLUTELY FOREVER DISCHARGES BUYER, EACH COMPANY AND THEIR RESPECTIVE SUBSIDIARIES (EACH, A “COMPANY
RELEASED PARTY”) FROM AND AGAINST ANY AND ALL COMPANY RELEASED MATTERS. “COMPANY RELEASED MATTERS” MEANS
ANY AND ALL CLAIMS, RIGHTS, LOSSES, DAMAGES, DEBTS, LIABILITIES, OBLIGATIONS, COSTS, EXPENSES (INCLUDING ATTORNEYS’ AND ACCOUNTANTS’
FEES AND EXPENSES), ACTIONS AND CAUSES OF ACTION OF ANY NATURE WHATSOEVER, WHETHER NOW KNOWN OR UNKNOWN, SUSPECTED OR UNSUSPECTED, THAT
SUCH SELLER RELEASING PARTIES (OR ANY OF THEM) NOW HAS, OR AT ANY TIME PREVIOUSLY HAD, OR SHALL OR MAY IN THE FUTURE HAVE, (I) AS
A SHAREHOLDER, MEMBER OR EQUITYHOLDER (WHETHER DIRECT OR INDIRECT) OF ANY COMPANY OR ANY OF ITS SUBSIDIARIES, ARISING BY VIRTUE OF OR
IN ANY MANNER RELATED TO THE OWNERSHIP, ORGANIZATION, MANAGEMENT OR OPERATION OF ANY COMPANY AND/OR ITS SUBSIDIARIES PRIOR TO THE CLOSING,
(II) ARISING OUT OF ANY INACCURACY OR BREACH OF ANY REPRESENTATION OR WARRANTY OF ANY COMPANY OR BUYER OR THE BREACH OF ANY COVENANT,
UNDERTAKING OR OTHER AGREEMENT THAT, BY ITS TERMS, IS REQUIRED TO BE PERFORMED PRIOR TO THE CLOSING CONTAINED IN THIS AGREEMENT,
THE RELATED AGREEMENTS AND THE DISCLOSURE SCHEDULE OR IN ANY CERTIFICATE CONTEMPLATED HEREBY AND DELIVERED IN CONNECTION HEREWITH, (III) ANY
INFORMATION, DOCUMENTS OR MATERIALS FURNISHED BY OR ON BEHALF OF BUYER OR ANY OF ITS AFFILIATES, OR (IV) ARISING OUT OF ANY DECISION
OR ACTION TAKEN OR THAT MAY BE TAKEN OR THAT MAY BE DECIDED TO BE TAKEN BY SELLER OR ANY OF ITS REPRESENTATIVES. SELLER IRREVOCABLY
COVENANTS AND AGREES NOT TO, DIRECTLY OR INDIRECTLY, AND COVENANTS AND AGREES TO CAUSE THE SELLER RELEASING PARTIES NOT TO, ASSERT, MAINTAIN
OR PURSUE ANY CLAIM, ASSERT ANY RIGHT AND/OR BRING OR MAINTAIN ANY LITIGATION WITH RESPECT TO, ARISING OUT OF, OR RELATING TO ANY COMPANY
RELEASED MATTER AGAINST THE COMPANY RELEASED PARTIES OR ANY OF THEM. NOTWITHSTANDING THE FOREGOING, EACH SELLER RELEASING PARTY RETAINS
AND MAINTAINS AND DOES NOT RELEASE, WAIVE OR DISCHARGE ANY CLAIMS, RIGHTS, LOSSES, DAMAGES, DEBTS, LIABILITIES, OBLIGATIONS, COSTS, EXPENSES
(INCLUDING ATTORNEYS’ AND ACCOUNTANTS’ FEES AND EXPENSES), ACTIONS AND CAUSES OF ACTION OF ANY NATURE WHATSOEVER, WHETHER
NOW KNOWN OR UNKNOWN, SUSPECTED OR UNSUSPECTED NOW OR IN THE FUTURE, WHETHER ARISING UNDER, OR BASED UPON, ANY LAW OR OTHERWISE, (I) TO
THE EXTENT ARISING FROM ANY COMMERCIAL ARRANGEMENT THAT IS NOT TERMINATED HEREUNDER BETWEEN SUCH SELLER RELEASING PARTY AND ANY COMPANY
(OR ANY OF THEIR RESPECTIVE AFFILIATES) OR ANY COMMERCIAL ARRANGEMENT BETWEEN SUCH SELLER RELEASING PARTY AND BUYER OR ITS SUBSIDIARIES,
(II) UNDER, BUT IN ANY CASE, SUBJECT TO THE TERMS OF, THIS AGREEMENT AND/OR ANY RELATED AGREEMENT (INCLUDING, SUBJECT TO THE TERMS
OF THIS AGREEMENT, ANY RIGHT TO INDEMNIFICATION UNDER ARTICLE 10) OR (III) FRAUD (AS DEFINED HEREIN). SELLER ACKNOWLEDGES
AND AGREES, ON BEHALF OF ITSELF AND THE OTHER SELLER RELEASING PARTIES THAT NO COMPANY RELEASED PARTY SHALL HAVE ANY SHARED OR VICARIOUS
LIABILITY, OR OTHERWISE BE THE SUBJECT OF LEGAL OR EQUITABLE CLAIMS, FOR THE ACTIONS, OMISSIONS OR FRAUD OF ANY OTHER PERSON. NOTWITHSTANDING
ANY PROVISION OF THIS AGREEMENT OR ANY RELATED AGREEMENT TO THE CONTRARY, THE COMPANY RELEASED PARTIES NOT PARTY TO THIS AGREEMENT ARE
EXPRESS, INTENDED THIRD-PARTY BENEFICIARIES OF THIS SECTION 7.5 WITH FULL RIGHTS OF ENFORCEMENT OF THIS SECTION 7.5
AS IF A PARTY HERETO.

(b)           EFFECTIVE
AS OF THE CLOSING, EACH OF BUYER AND EACH COMPANY DOES FOR ITSELF AND ITS AFFILIATES, SUCCESSORS AND ASSIGNS, IF ANY, AND THEIR
RESPECTIVE HEIRS, BENEFICIARIES, SUCCESSORS AND ASSIGNS (EACH, A “BUYER RELEASING PARTY”), VOLUNTARILY, IRREVOCABLY,

AND ACCOUNTANTS’ FEES AND EXPENSES), ACTIONS AND CAUSES OF ACTION OF ANY NATURE WHATSOEVER, WHETHER NOW KNOWN OR UNKNOWN, SUSPECTED
OR UNSUSPECTED, THAT SUCH BUYER RELEASING PARTIES (OR ANY OF THEM) NOW HAS, OR AT ANY TIME PREVIOUSLY HAD, OR SHALL OR MAY IN THE
FUTURE HAVE, ARISING OUT OF (I) THE OWNERSHIP, ORGANIZATION, MANAGEMENT OR OPERATION OF THE BUSINESSES OF THE COMPANIES AND THEIR
SUBSIDIARIES ON OR PRIOR TO THE CLOSING, (II) ANY INACCURACY OR BREACH OF ANY REPRESENTATION OR WARRANTY OF SELLER OR THE COMPANIES
OR THE BREACH OF ANY COVENANT, UNDERTAKING OR OTHER AGREEMENT THAT, BY ITS TERMS, IS REQUIRED TO BE PERFORMED PRIOR TO THE CLOSING
CONTAINED IN THIS AGREEMENT, THE RELATED AGREEMENTS AND THE DISCLOSURE SCHEDULES OR IN ANY CERTIFICATE CONTEMPLATED HEREBY AND DELIVERED
IN CONNECTION HEREWITH, (III) ANY INFORMATION, DOCUMENTS OR MATERIALS FURNISHED BY OR ON BEHALF OF SELLER OR ANY OF ITS AFFILIATES,
ANY COMPANY OR ANY SUBSIDIARY OF ANY COMPANY, OR (IV) ANY DECISION OR ACTION TAKEN OR THAT MAY BE TAKEN OR THAT MAY BE
DECIDED TO BE TAKEN BY BUYER OR ANY OF ITS REPRESENTATIVES. EACH OF BUYER AND EACH COMPANY IRREVOCABLY COVENANTS AND AGREES NOT TO, DIRECTLY
OR INDIRECTLY, AND COVENANTS AND AGREES TO CAUSE ITS RESPECTIVE AFFILIATES (INCLUDING THE COMPANY AND ITS SUBSIDIARIES), HEIRS, LEGAL
REPRESENTATIVES, SUCCESSORS AND ASSIGNS NOT TO, ASSERT, MAINTAIN OR PURSUE ANY CLAIM, ASSERT ANY RIGHT AND/OR BRING OR MAINTAIN ANY LITIGATION
WITH RESPECT TO, ARISING OUT OF, OR RELATING TO ANY SELLER RELEASED MATTER AGAINST THE SELLER RELEASED PARTIES OR ANY OF THEM. NOTWITHSTANDING
THE FOREGOING, EACH OF BUYER RELEASING PARTY RETAINS AND MAINTAINS AND DOES NOT RELEASE OR WAIVE ITS RIGHTS TO ENFORCE THE OBLIGATIONS
OF SELLER NOW OR IN THE FUTURE, WHETHER ARISING UNDER, OR BASED UPON, ANY LAW OR OTHERWISE, (I) TO THE EXTENT ARISING FROM ANY COMMERCIAL

and Buyer shall reasonably cooperate, and shall cause their respective Representatives to reasonably cooperate, at the expense of the
requesting party, in preparing and filing all Tax Returns of the Companies, relating to any Pre-Closing Period or Straddle Period (except

and audits with respect to all such periods ending on or before the Closing Date and Straddle Periods. Notwithstanding anything to the

(c)           If
Buyer, or any of its Affiliates or any of the Companies or their Subsidiaries, on the one hand, or Seller, or any of its Affiliates,
on the other hand, receives written notice of any audit, investigation, or other action by a Taxing Authority in respect of any Tax Return
of any of the Companies or their Subsidiaries for a Pre-Closing Period other than a Seller Group Tax Return (a “Pre-Closing
Tax Audit”), then such party will give written notice to the other party promptly after receiving such notice. Buyer shall
have the right, at its own expense, to control the defense of any Pre-Closing Tax Audit; provided, that Buyer shall (x) keep
Seller reasonably informed of all material matters that come to its attention in respect of the Pre-Closing Tax Audit, (y) permit
Seller to participate, at its own expense, in any such Pre-Closing Tax Audit and (z) not settle or compromise such Pre Closing Tax
Audit without the consent of Seller (not to be unreasonably withheld, conditioned or delayed) to the extent that such settlement or compromise
could reasonably be expected to increase the Taxes of Seller or any of its Affiliates or result in any Liability for Seller under this
Agreement. The Parties understand and agree that in the event any Pre-Closing Tax Audit after the Closing Date results in an adjustment
under Section 6225 of the Code where the reviewed year (as defined in Section 6225(d) of the Code) is or includes a Pre-Closing
Tax Period, solely to the extent that such entire adjustment under Section 6225 of the Code is not covered under the R&W Insurance
Policy, the Seller shall reasonably cooperate with Buyer to make an election under Section 6226 of the Code. Buyer shall (A) notify
the insurer providing coverage under the R&W Insurance Policy (1) of any Pre-Closing Tax Audit related to a Subsidiary of a
Company that is governed by the Bipartisan Budget Act of 2015 within fifteen (15) days of becoming aware of such Pre-Closing Tax Audit
and (2) of any “Notice of Proposed Partnership Adjustment” (or similar notification) within five (5) days of receipt
of such notice and (B) use commercially reasonable efforts to make a claim and comply with any other R&W Insurance policy requirements
to obtain coverage for such “imputed underpayment” under the R&W Insurance Policy. Seller shall have the right, but not
the obligation, to control any Pre-Closing Tax Audit of any Subsidiary of a Company that is governed by the Bipartisan Budget Act of
2015 for which Buyer is expected to cause an election under Section 6226 of the Code to be made; provided, that Seller shall
(w) notify Buyer in writing of its decision to assume control of any such Pre-Closing Tax Audit, (x) keep Buyer reasonably
informed of all material matters that come to its attention in respect of the such Pre-Closing Tax Audit, (y) permit Buyer to participate,
at its own expense, in any such Pre-Closing Tax Audit and (z) not settle or compromise such Pre Closing Tax Audit without the consent
of Buyer (not to be unreasonably withheld, conditioned or delayed). This Section 7.10(c) and Section 7.10(f),
as applicable, shall control all audits, investigations, or other actions by a Taxing Authority with respect to Taxes, notwithstanding
anything in this Agreement to the contrary.

(g)            Buyer
shall not, and shall not permit any of its Affiliates to (including the Companies and their Subsidiaries after the Closing Date), without
the prior written consent of Seller, which consent may not be unreasonably withheld, conditioned or delayed, (i) file, amend, re-file
or otherwise modify any Tax Return relating in whole or in part to the Companies or any current or former Subsidiary with respect to
any Pre-Closing Period, including the filings of Tax Returns for a Pre-Closing Period in a jurisdiction where the Companies (and their
Subsidiaries) have not historically filed Tax Returns, (ii) initiate discussions or examinations or file any ruling or request with
any Governmental Authority regarding Taxes of the Companies (and their Subsidiaries) with respect to any Pre-Closing Period, (iii) make
any voluntary disclosures with respect to Taxes of the Companies (and their Subsidiaries) for Pre-Closing Periods, (iv) change any
accounting method or adopt any convention that shifts Taxable income of the Companies (and their Subsidiaries) from a period beginning
(or deemed to begin) after the Closing Date to a Taxable period (or portion thereof) ending on or prior to the Closing Date or shifts
deductions or losses from a Pre-Closing Period to a period beginning (or deemed to begin) after the Closing Date, (v) make any Tax
election for any Company (or any of Subsidiary) that has retroactive effect to any Pre-Closing Period (including an election under Section 6221
of the Code but excluding an election pursuant to Section 6226 of the Code) (vi) undertake any action (or cause any Company
or its Subsidiaries to undertake any action) outside of the ordinary course of business on the Closing Date after the Closing, in each
case (other than clause (v)), to the extent that such settlement or compromise could reasonably be expected to increase the Taxes of
Seller or any of its Affiliates (other than the Companies or their Subsidiaries) or result in any Liability for Seller under this Agreement.
Neither Buyer nor any of its Affiliates shall, subject to Section 7.10(h), make any election under Section 338 of the
Code (or any similar provision under state, local, or foreign Law) with respect to the acquisition of the Companies or any of their Subsidiaries,
without the consent of Seller (which may be withheld in its sole discretion).
 116 
(h)            Notwithstanding
anything to the contrary in the foregoing, Buyer shall be entitled to make an election under Section 338(g) of the Code (or
any similar provision under state, local or foreign Law), solely with respect to any Company or Subsidiary of a Company that is not a
domestic corporation for U.S. federal income Tax purposes. if Buyer pays Seller the increased amount (if any) necessary to leave Seller
in the same after-Tax position as if such election had not been made, as determined on a “with” and “without basis”
(the “Section 338(g) Payment”). If Buyer notifies Seller of its desire to make such election, (a) Seller
shall reasonably cooperate with Buyer to provide information necessary to determine the impact of such election on its after-Tax position
and (b) Buyer shall promptly remit the Section 338(g) Payment prior to the date in which such election is required to
be filed with the appropriate Taxing authority. In connection with the foregoing, the Parties agree to allocate the “adjusted grossed
up basis” (within the meaning of the Treasury Regulations under Section 338 of the Code) in a manner consistent with, and
subject to the principles of Section 2.5.
(i)             Any
Transaction Tax Deductions will, to the maximum extent permitted by Law (applying a “more likely than not” standard), be
allocated for Tax reporting purposes to Pre-Closing Periods and shall be reported on a Seller Group Tax Return.
(j)             Seller
and its Affiliates shall cause the Companies and their Subsidiaries to be excluded from Seller’s or its Affiliates’ Tax Sharing
Agreements to which any of the Companies or their Subsidiaries are a party as of the Closing Date, and after the Closing Date, none of
the Companies or their Subsidiaries shall have any liability to Seller or its Affiliates under the foregoing.
(k)            For
Taxes that are imposed on a periodic basis and are payable for a Straddle Period, the portion of such Tax that relates to the pre-Closing
portion of the Straddle Period will be: (i) in the case of payroll Taxes and Taxes based upon or related to income or receipts,
equal the amount that would be payable if the relevant Tax period ended on the Closing Date; provided, however, that all permitted
allowances, exemptions and deductions that are normally computed on the basis of an entire year or period (such as depreciation), will
be allocated between the pre-Closing portion of the Straddle Period and the post-Closing portion of the Straddle Period pro rata according
to the number of calendar days in each period; and (ii) in the case of all other Taxes, equal to the amount of such Tax for the
entire Tax period multiplied by the number of days in the Tax period ending on the Closing Date and divided by the number of days in
the entire Tax period. The portion of any such other Tax allocated to the post-Closing portion of a Straddle Period will equal the balance
of the Tax attributable to the Straddle Period.
(l)             For
the purposes of this Section 7.10(l): words and phrases which have a defined meaning in the GST Act have the same meaning
when used in this Section 7.10(l), unless the contrary intention appears; and each periodic or progressive component of a
supply to which section 156-5(1) of the GST Act applies is to be treated as if it were a separate supply.
 117 
(i)           Unless
expressly stated otherwise, all consideration to be provided under this document is exclusive of GST.
(ii)          If
GST is payable, or notionally payable, on a supply made in connection with this document, the party providing the consideration for the
supply agrees to pay to the supplier an additional amount equal to the amount of GST payable on that supply (“GST Amount”).
(iii)        Subject
to the prior receipt of a tax invoice, the GST Amount is payable at the same time as the GST-exclusive consideration for the supply,
or the first part of the GST-exclusive consideration for the supply (as the case may be), is payable or is to be provided.
(iv)        This
Section 7.10(l) does not apply to the extent that the consideration for the supply is expressly stated to include GST
or the supply is subject to a reverse-charge.
(v)         If
an adjustment event arises for a supply made in connection with this document, the GST Amount (or the GST component of any consideration
expressed to be inclusive of GST) must be recalculated to reflect that adjustment. The supplier or the party providing the consideration
for the supply (as the case may be) agrees to make any payments necessary to reflect the adjustment and the supplier agrees to issue
an adjustment note.
(vi)        Unless
expressly stated otherwise in this document, any payment, indemnity, reimbursement or similar obligation that is required to be made
in connection with this document which is calculated by reference to an amount paid by another party must be reduced by the amount of
any Input Tax Credits which the other party (or the representative member of any GST group of which the other party is a member) is entitled.
If the reduced payment is consideration for a taxable supply, clauses 11.21(c)-(e) apply to the reduced payment.

Each of Seller and Buyer acknowledges that the information being provided to it in connection with the transactions contemplated hereby

Confidentiality Agreement from any Liability for any breach of the Confidentiality Agreement prior to such replacement.

7.13          Insurance.
From and after the Closing Date, the Companies shall cease to be insured by, have access or availability to, be entitled to make claims
on, be entitled to claim benefits from or seek coverage under any policy of or agreement for insurance and interests in insurance pools
and programs covering risks of Seller or its Affiliates (in each case including self-insurance and insurance from such an Affiliate)
(“Seller Insurance”) and all rights of any nature with respect to any of the foregoing, including in each case all
recoveries thereunder and rights to assert claims seeking any such recoveries. For any loss occurrence taking place prior to the Closing
for which the Companies have claimed benefit, sought coverage or received payment under any Seller Insurance, and such loss has not been
settled and discharged between the Companies and Seller on or prior to the Closing, the Companies shall reimburse Seller within thirty
(30) days of receipt of an invoice for any costs, charges, retention, deductible, retrospective premium, cash payment for reserves calculated
or charged on an incurred loss basis or other similar items and losses (including associated administrative expenses and allocated loss
adjustment or similar expenses) incurred by or allocated to Seller. In the event that Buyer or its Affiliates (including, after the Closing,
the Companies) receives any payment under any Seller Insurance on behalf of any business of Seller or any of its Affiliates after the
Closing Date, such payments shall be the property of, and shall be forwarded and remitted to, Seller as promptly as practicable, but
not later than twenty (20) days, after receipt thereof (or, if later, after notice or determination of receipt of such improper payment).
Notwithstanding anything to the contrary contained herein, Buyer acknowledges that Seller is not responsible for procuring or maintaining
insurance coverage with respect to the post-Closing conduct or activities of the Companies or the businesses, assets or properties of
the Companies, including with respect to any Undisclosed Contracts.
7.14          Lock-Up.
(a)            Subject
to Section 7.14(b) and Section 7.14(c), during the period commencing on the Closing Date and ending
at 11:59 p.m. Eastern Time on the date that is twelve (12) months after the Closing Date (the “Lock-Up Period”),
Seller shall not directly or indirectly sell or transfer any Acquirer Shares issued and delivered pursuant to Section 2.3(a) that
are then held by Seller (the “Restricted Shares”); provided that nothing in this Section 7.14 shall
restrict Seller from (i) entering into, establishing, maintaining or otherwise engaging in any hedging or other similar transaction
or arrangement (including swaps, collars, put/call positions, forward or other derivative transactions) with respect to the Restricted
Shares, (ii) pledging Restricted Shares as collateral to secure any obligations under, or in connection with, any such bona fide
hedging transaction, or in a margin or similar account or (iii) taking any action to establish any of the foregoing, in each case
so long as settlement of any such transaction, position or pledge does not require the sale or transfer of any Restricted Shares during
the Lock-Up Period.
(b)            Notwithstanding
anything in Section 7.14(a) to the contrary, Seller may transfer Restricted Shares (i) to one or more Affiliates,
or (ii) pursuant to a Governmental Order or by operation of Law, provided that, in each case, each transferee agrees in writing
to be bound by the restrictions applicable to Seller set forth in Section 7.14(a) for the remainder of the Lock-Up Period,
in each case subject to the exceptions set forth in this Section 7.14(b) and Section 7.14(c).
 119 
(c)            Notwithstanding
the foregoing, the restrictions in Section 7.14(a) shall not apply to any sale, transfer or exchange made pursuant
to or in connection with any merger, tender offer, reclassification, reorganization, recapitalization, consolidation, stock exchange
or similar transaction involving the Acquirer Shares made to all holders of Acquirer Shares that would result in the holders of Acquirer
Shares exchanging their shares for cash, securities or other property.

7.17         Removal
of Confidential Information. Promptly after Closing, Buyer shall use commercially reasonable efforts to, and shall cause the Companies
and their Subsidiaries to use commercially reasonable efforts to, (a) conduct a reasonable and good-faith search of all information
technology systems, servers, databases, shared drives, cloud storage environments, email systems, and other electronic repositories (collectively,
the “Target Systems”) to identify any and all Confidential Information of Seller residing therein, excluding any Confidential
Information that (i) Buyer or any of its Subsidiaries is entitled to access under any of the Related Agreement or (ii) is required
to be retained by applicable Law in accordance with the terms of the confidentiality obligations under the Restrictive Covenant Agreement,
and (b) permanently delete, destroy, or render unrecoverable all such Confidential Information from the Target Systems, in each
case using commercially reasonable, industry-standard data deletion methods. Buyer shall direct all of the employees, agents, and subcontractors
of the Companies and their Subsidiaries who had access to Seller’s Confidential Information to similarly delete and destroy all
such information in their possession or control. Seller shall reasonably cooperate with Buyer to identify Seller Confidential Information
residing on the Target Systems, including by designating a representative to assist Buyer in distinguishing Seller Confidential Information
from information properly relating to the businesses of the Companies and their Subsidiaries. Buyer shall provide Seller and its designated
representatives with reasonable access to the Target Systems (on a read-only basis and subject to reasonable confidentiality and security
protocols) solely for purposes of facilitating such identification and verifying deletion in accordance with this Section 7.17.

conditions as of the Closing Date:

8.1(a) of the Disclosure Schedule (together, the “Required Regulatory Approvals”) shall have been obtained
and remain in full force and effect (including (x) through the lapse, without objection of a prescribed time under a statute or
regulation that states that a transaction may be implemented if a prescribed time lapses following the giving of a notice without an
objection being made, or (y) upon any written decision by any such applicable Governmental Authority declining its jurisdiction
to issue a Required Regulatory Approval).
(b)            No
Prohibition. No Governmental Order shall have been adopted, promulgated or entered by any Governmental Authority that is in effect
and that prohibits the consummation of the transactions contemplated hereby.

to the satisfaction (or waiver by Buyer) of each of the following conditions precedent:

ARTICLE 9
Termination
9.1           Termination.
This Agreement may be terminated at any time on or prior to the Closing Date, as follows:
(a)            with
the mutual written consent of Seller and Buyer; or
(b)            by
either Seller or Buyer, if the Closing shall not have occurred on or before May 9, 2027, (the “Termination Date”);
provided, however, that if, as of the Termination Date, all conditions set forth in Article 8, other than the conditions
set forth in Section 8.1(a) or Section 8.1(b) (to the extent related to Section 8.1(a)),
shall have been satisfied or shall be capable of being satisfied on the Closing Date were the Closing to occur at such time, or to the
extent not prohibited by applicable Law shall have been waived on or before such date, either Buyer or Seller may extend the Termination
Date by up to three (3) months, and such date thereafter shall be deemed to be the Termination Date; provided, further, that
the right to terminate this Agreement under this Section 9.1(b) shall not be available to (i) any Party whose (or
whose Affiliates) failure to perform any obligation or satisfy any condition under this Agreement has been the cause of, resulted in
or contributed to the failure of the Closing to occur on or before the Termination Date (as it may be extended pursuant to this Section 9.1(b))
or (ii) Buyer if the Companies or Seller are pursuing an action that seeks to specifically enforce the performance of Buyer’s
obligations under this Agreement to consummate the Closing or other similar equitable relief; or
(c)            by
Seller, if Buyer shall have breached or failed to perform in any material respect any of its covenants in this Agreement, which breach
or failure to perform (i) would give rise to the failure of a condition set forth in Section 8.1 or Section 8.3
being satisfied and (ii) (A) if capable of being cured, has not been cured by Buyer within thirty (30) days after its receipt
of written notice thereof from Seller (provided that if Seller delivers such written notice within thirty (30) days of the Termination
Date, the Termination Date shall automatically be extended to a date that is thirty (30) days after the date of such delivery); or (B) is
incapable of being cured provided, however, that the right to terminate this Agreement pursuant to this Section 9.1(c) shall
not be available to Seller if the failure of any such condition was caused (or materially contributed to) by Seller or the Companies;
or
 124 
(d)            by
Buyer, if Seller shall have breached or failed to perform in any material respect any of its covenants in this Agreement, which breach
or failure to perform (i) would give rise to the failure of a condition set forth in Section 8.1 or Section 8.2
being satisfied and (ii) (A) if capable of being cured, has not been cured by Seller within thirty (30) days after their receipt
of written notice thereof from Buyer (provided that if Buyer delivers such written notice within thirty (30) days of the Termination
Date, the Termination Date shall automatically be extended to a date that is thirty (30) days after the date of such delivery); or (B) is
incapable of being cured; provided, however, that the right to terminate this Agreement pursuant to this Section 9.1(d) shall
not be available to Buyer if the failure of any such condition was caused (or materially contributed to) by Buyer; or
(e)            by
Seller if (i) all of the conditions set forth in Section 8.1 and Section 8.2 have been and continue to be
satisfied or shall be capable of being satisfied at the Closing Date were the Closing to occur at such time and (ii) Buyer fails
to consummate the transactions contemplated by this Agreement within two (2) Business Days of the date that the Closing should otherwise
have occurred pursuant to Section 2.2 hereof and (iii) Seller and the Companies stood ready and willing to consummate
the Closing on the date the Closing should otherwise have occurred pursuant to Section 2.2;
(f)             by
either Seller or Buyer, if any Governmental Authority shall have issued a Governmental Order that permanently restrains, enjoins or otherwise
prohibits the transactions contemplated by this Agreement, and such Governmental Order shall have become final and non-appealable; provided,
however, that the right to terminate this Agreement under this Section 9.1(f) shall not be available to any Party
whose (or whose Affiliate’s) failure to comply with Section 6.4(a) or Section 6.6 has caused, resulted
in or contributed to, such action or inaction; or
(g)            by
Seller, if a Buyer Material Adverse Effect under clause (a) of the definition thereof shall have occurred; provided, however,
that the right to terminate this Agreement under this Section 9.1(g) shall not be available to Seller, with respect
to any specific occurrence of a Buyer Material Adverse Effect under clause (a) of the definition thereof, following the date
that is five (5) Business Days after the date on which Buyer notifies Seller in writing that such specific Buyer Material Adverse
Effect under clause (a) of the definition thereof has occurred.
9.2            Effect
of Termination. In the event of termination of this Agreement by either (a) Seller or (b) Buyer as provided in Section 9.1,
this Agreement shall become void and have no further force or effect, without any Liability on the part of Buyer, Seller or the Companies;
provided, however, that the provisions of Section 6.1(b), Section 7.3, this Section 9.2 and
ARTICLE 11 will survive any termination hereof; provided, further, that nothing in this Section 9.2 shall
relieve Seller, the Companies or Buyer of any Liability for any Willful Breach prior to termination. The Confidentiality Agreement will
survive any termination hereof in accordance with its terms.
9.3            Notice
of Termination. In order to validly terminate this Agreement pursuant to Section 9.1, the Party seeking to terminate
this Agreement shall deliver written notice to the other Parties, specifying (i) the subsection of Section 9.1 pursuant
to which this Agreement is being terminated and (ii) the factual basis, in reasonable detail, giving rise to such Party’s
right to terminate this Agreement pursuant to Section 9.1.
